\displaystyle\lesssim\tfrac{N_{1}^{\beta_{1}}N_{2}^{\beta_{2}}N_{3}^{\beta_{3}}N_{4}^{\beta_{4}}}{(\kappa+N_{1})^{2}(\kappa+N_{2})^{2}(\kappa+N_{3})^{2}(\kappa+N_{4})^{2}}\|(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}\|(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\|(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\|\big(\psi^{3}\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\big)_{N_{4}}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}.

We then sum over the lowest two frequencies and invoke the estimates laid out above. When
N_{4}\leq N_{2}
, this leads to a bound of the form

\sum_{N_{1}\geq N_{2}}\Gamma_{N_{1},N_{2}}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2}

where
\Gamma
is the matrix

\displaystyle\Gamma_{N_{1},N_{2}}:=|\varkappa|^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}

\displaystyle(|\varkappa|+N_{2})^{s-\frac{1}{2}}\kappa^{-8}N_{1}^{\beta_{1}-\frac{5}{2}-s}N_{2}^{\beta_{2}-\frac{3}{2}-3s}

\displaystyle\times\min\{N_{1}^{\frac{5}{2}},\kappa N_{1},\kappa^{2}\}\min\{N_{2}^{\beta_{3}+\beta_{4}+\frac{5}{2}},\kappa N_{2}^{\beta_{3}+\beta_{4}+1},\kappa^{\beta_{3}+\beta_{4}+2}\}.

When on the other hand
N_{4}>N_{2}
, we are lead to a bound of the form

\sum_{N_{1}\geq N_{4}}\Gamma_{N_{1},N_{4}}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2}+\sum_{N_{4}\geq N_{1}}\Bigl[\tfrac{|\varkappa|+N_{4}}{|\varkappa|+N_{1}}\Bigr]^{s-\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma_{N_{4},N_{1}}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2},

with corresponding permutations of the indices
\beta
.

In this way, we see that the proof can be completed by proving

\sum_{N\geq M}\Gamma_{N,M}\lesssim\kappa^{|\beta|-7-\frac{8}{3}(s+\frac{1}{2})}.

As the matrix entries are monotone in
|\varkappa|
, it suffices to prove the bound when
|\varkappa|=\kappa^{2/3}
. Summing first in
N
, we are left to estimate

\displaystyle\sum_{M\geq 1}\kappa^{-8-\frac{1}{3}(2s+1)}(\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}}+M

\displaystyle)^{s-\frac{1}{2}}M^{\beta_{2}-\frac{3}{2}(2s+1)}\min\Bigl\{M^{\beta_{3}+\beta_{4}+\frac{5}{2}}[\kappa^{\beta_{1}-\frac{1}{2}-s}+\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}(\beta_{1}-s)}],

(5.26)

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}M^{\beta_{3}+\beta_{4}+1}[\kappa^{\beta_{1}-\frac{3}{2}-s}+M^{\beta_{1}-\frac{3}{2}-s}],\ \kappa^{\beta_{3}+\beta_{4}+4}M^{\beta_{1}-\frac{5}{2}-s}\Bigr\}.

From here, one need only consider the cases
M\leq\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}}
,
\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}}\leq M\leq\kappa
, and
M\geq\kappa
.
∎

For the mKdV we have the following variation:

Lemma 5.11

(Quartic estimates for the mKdV)
.

Let
m\in S(\beta;\kappa)
with
1\leq|\beta|\leq 8
. For any
|\varkappa|\geq\sqrt{\kappa}\geq 1
and any Schwartz functions
q,f\in\mathcal{C}([-1,1];B_{\delta}\cap\mathscr{S})
satisfying

{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|f\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}\lesssim{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}},

we have the paraproduct estimate

(5.27)

\displaystyle\bigl\|m[q,r,q,\psi_{h}^{12}

\displaystyle\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}]\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\bigl\|m[q,r,q,\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}]\,\psi_{h}^{12}\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big[\tfrac{\kappa^{-2s}}{|\varkappa|}+|\varkappa|^{-2(2s+1)}\log\bigl|\tfrac{4\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa}\bigr|\Big]\kappa^{|\beta|-8}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},

where the implicit constant is independent of
\kappa,\varkappa,
and
h\in\mathbb{R}
. Moreover, if
|\beta|\geq 2
,

(5.28)

\displaystyle\biggl|\iint m\bigl[q,r,q,\psi_{h}^{12}

\displaystyle\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]\,dx\,dt\biggr|\lesssim\kappa^{|\beta|-9-2s}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

Remark
.

As we will see in the proof, it is not essential that the first three entries in the paraproduct are exactly
q
,
r
, and
q
. Rather, we only require that they obey the same estimates as
q
, in the manner that
f
does. As we shall seldom need this extra generality, we have chosen to present the lemma in this more representative form.

Proof.

The proof is essentially identical to that of Lemma
5.10
. By space-translation symmetry, we may assume
h=0
.

We will reuse the
L^{\infty}_{t,x}
bounds appearing in the proof of Lemma
5.10
; however, the
L^{2}_{t,x}
local smoothing bounds used to treat the high-frequency terms must be adapted to the mKdV setting. Specifically, we will use

\displaystyle\tfrac{N^{j}}{(\kappa+N)^{2}}\|(\psi^{3}q)_{N}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-2}N^{j-3-s}\min\{N^{3},\kappa N,\kappa^{2}\}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}},

\displaystyle\tfrac{N^{j}}{(\kappa+N)^{2}}\|\big(\psi^{3}\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\big)_{N}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}(|\varkappa|+N)^{s-\frac{1}{2}}\kappa^{-2}N^{j-3-s}\min\{N^{3},\kappa N,\kappa^{2}\}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}.

Proceeding as in the proof of (
5.25
) we take

\displaystyle m_{N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},N_{4}}:=m\bigl[\psi^{-3}(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{1}},\psi^{-3}(\psi^{3}r)_{N_{2}},\psi^{-3}(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{3}},\psi^{9}(\psi^{3}\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial})_{N_{4}}\bigr],

so that

\|m[q,r,q,\psi^{12}\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}]\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\leq\sum\limits_{N_{j}\geq 1}\|m_{N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},N_{4}}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}},

or

\displaystyle\tilde{m}_{N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},N_{4}}:=m\bigl[\psi^{-3}(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{1}},\psi^{-3}(\psi^{3}r)_{N_{2}},\psi^{-3}(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{3}},\psi^{-3}(\psi^{3}\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial})_{N_{4}}\bigr],

\|m[q,r,q,\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}]\,\psi^{12}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\leq\sum\limits_{N_{j}\geq 1}\|\tilde{m}_{N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},N_{4}}\,\psi^{12}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}.

As in the proof of (
5.25
), it suffices to restrict our attention to the case
N_{1}\geq N_{2}\geq N_{3}\geq N_{4}
. With
\ell=3,9
, we may bound

LHS
(
5.27
)

\displaystyle\lesssim\!\!\sum\limits_{N_{1}\geq N_{2}\geq N_{3}\geq N_{4}}\!\!\|\psi^{3}\tfrac{\partial^{\beta_{1}}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\psi^{-3}(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}\|\psi^{3}\tfrac{\partial^{\beta_{2}}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\psi^{-3}(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\times\|\psi^{3}\tfrac{\partial^{\beta_{3}}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\psi^{-3}(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\|\psi^{-\ell}\tfrac{\partial^{\beta_{4}}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\psi^{\ell}(\psi^{3}\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial})_{N_{4}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\!\!\!\sum\limits_{N_{1}\geq N_{2}\geq N_{3}\geq N_{4}}\!\!\!\!\tfrac{N_{1}^{\beta_{1}}N_{2}^{\beta_{2}}N_{3}^{\beta_{3}}N_{4}^{\beta_{4}}}{(\kappa+N_{1})^{2}(\kappa+N_{2})^{2}(\kappa+N_{3})^{2}(\kappa+N_{4})^{2}}\|(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}\|(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\times\|(\psi^{3}q)_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\|(\psi^{3}\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial})_{N_{4}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}.

Summing in
N_{3}\geq N_{4}\geq 1
we obtain a bound of a constant multiple of

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{N_{1}\geq N_{2}}|\varkappa|^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}(|\varkappa|+N_{2})^{s-\frac{1}{2}}\kappa^{-8}N_{1}^{\beta_{1}-3-s}N_{2}^{\beta_{2}-2-3s}\min\{N_{1}^{3},\kappa N_{1},\kappa^{2}\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\min\{N_{2}^{\beta_{3}+\beta_{4}+3},\kappa N_{2}^{\beta_{3}+\beta_{4}+1},\kappa^{\beta_{3}+\beta_{4}+2}\}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

Proceeding as in Lemma
5.10
and summing in
N_{1}
, we are led to control the following analogue of (
5
):

\displaystyle\sum_{M\geq 1}\kappa^{-8}|\varkappa|^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}(|\varkappa|

\displaystyle+M)^{s-\frac{1}{2}}M^{\beta_{2}-2-3s}\min\Bigl\{M^{\beta_{3}+\beta_{4}+3}[\kappa^{\beta_{1}-1-s}+\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}(\beta_{1}-s)}],

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}M^{\beta_{3}+\beta_{4}+1}[\kappa^{\beta_{1}-2-s}+M^{\beta_{1}-2-s}],\ \kappa^{\beta_{3}+\beta_{4}+4}M^{\beta_{1}-3-s}\Bigr\}.

Once again, this requires consideration of individual cases. Unlike in Lemma
5.10
, the final bound depends upon
|\varkappa|
and so we cannot exploit monotonicity; thus, we need to treat separately
\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq|\varkappa|\leq\kappa
and
|\varkappa|\geq\kappa
. Evaluating these sums carefully reveals that
(
5.27
) can be improved to

(5.29)

\displaystyle\text{LHS\eqref{mKdV-para-2}}\lesssim\Big[\tfrac{\kappa^{-2s}}{\kappa+|\varkappa|}+|\varkappa|^{-2(2s+1)}\log\bigl|\tfrac{4\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa}\bigr|\Big]\kappa^{|\beta|-8}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}

in two cases: (i) if
|\beta|\geq 3
or (ii) if
|\beta|=2
and no individual
\beta_{j}=2
. These bounds suffice to prove (
5.28
) because if
|\beta|=2
and some factor has two derivatives (i.e. some
\beta_{j}=2
), then we may integrate by parts to redistribute one of the derivatives and recover case (ii).
∎

Next we prove another pair of lemmas that will act as replacements for Lemmas
2.6
,
2.7
in certain situations:

Lemma 5.12
.

Let
|\varkappa|\geq\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}}\geq 1
and
f_{1},f_{2}\in\mathcal{C}([-1,1];B_{\delta}\cap\mathscr{S})
satisfy

{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|f_{j}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}\lesssim{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}.

Then we have the estimate

(5.30)

\|(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi_{h}f_{1}\cdot\psi_{h}\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial})(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{2}}^{2}\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-3}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{8s}{3}}+|\varkappa|^{-4s}\bigr]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

Proof.

By translation invariance, we may take
h=0
. Decomposing dyadically and using (
2.22
), yields

\mathrm{LHS}\eqref{L4I4-replacement NLS}\lesssim\sum_{N\geq 1}(|\varkappa|+N)^{-1}\log\big(4+\tfrac{N^{2}}{|\varkappa|^{2}}\big)\bigl\|P_{N}(\psi f_{1}\cdot\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}^{2},

in which we then substitute the bound

\bigl\|P_{N}(\psi f_{1}\cdot\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}\leq\biggl\|\sum_{N_{1},N_{2}\geq 1}\big\|P_{N}\big((\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}\biggr\|_{L^{2}_{t}}.

We then proceed using the Littlewood–Paley trichotomy:

Case 1:
N_{2}\ll N_{1}\approx N
.
Applying Bernstein’s inequality, we bound

\displaystyle\big\|P_{N}\big((\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}\|\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N_{1}^{-s-\frac{3}{2}}\min\{N_{1}^{\frac{3}{2}},\kappa+N_{1}\}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}-s}(|\varkappa|+N_{2})^{-1}\delta{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}.

Observing that for fixed
N\geq 1
we have

\sum_{1\leq N_{2}\lesssim N}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}-s}(|\varkappa|+N_{2})^{-1}\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-1}(N\wedge|\varkappa|)^{\frac{1}{2}-s},

we are lead to estimate

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{N\geq 1}|\varkappa|^{-2}(N\wedge|\varkappa|)^{1-2s}(|\varkappa|+N)^{-1}\log(4+\tfrac{N^{2}}{|\varkappa|^{2}})N^{-2s-3}\min\{N^{3},(\kappa+N)^{2}\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-3}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{8s}{3}}+|\varkappa|^{-4s}\bigr].

Case 2:
N_{1}\ll N_{2}\approx N
.
A similar argument yields the estimate

\displaystyle\big\|P_{N}\big((\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}\lesssim N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}-s}(|\varkappa|+N_{2})^{-1}N_{2}^{-s-\frac{3}{2}}\min\{N_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}},\kappa+N_{2}\}\delta{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}.

This then leads us to evaluate

\sum_{N\geq 1}(|\varkappa|+N)^{-3}\log(4+\tfrac{N^{2}}{\varkappa^{2}})N^{-2(1+2s)}\min\{N^{3},(\kappa+N)^{2}\},

which yields the same bound as in Case 1.

Case 3:
N_{1}\approx N_{2}\gtrsim N
.
Bernstein’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\big\|P_{N}\big((\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\cdot

\displaystyle\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\big\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{\frac{1}{2}}\big\|(\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot N_{1}^{-s}(|\varkappa|+N_{1})^{-1}\big\|(2\varkappa-\partial)\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\big\|_{H^{s}}.

Thus, applying Cauchy–Schwarz to the sum, we obtain

\displaystyle\biggl\|\sum_{N_{1}\approx N_{2}\gtrsim N}\big\|P_{N}\big((\psi

\displaystyle f_{1})_{N_{1}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}\biggr\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim N\sum\limits_{N_{1}\gtrsim N}N_{1}^{-4s-3}(|\varkappa|+N_{1})^{-2}\min\{N_{1}^{3},(\kappa+N_{1})^{2}\}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

We are then left to evaluate the sum

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{N\geq 1}\sum_{N_{1}\gtrsim N}\tfrac{N}{|\varkappa|+N}\log\big(4+\tfrac{N^{2}}{|\varkappa|^{2}}\big)N_{1}^{-4s-3}(|\varkappa|+N_{1})^{-2}\min\{N_{1}^{3},(\kappa+N_{1})^{2}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum\limits_{N_{1}\geq 1}|\varkappa|^{-1}(|\varkappa|+N_{1})^{-2}N_{1}^{-4s-2}\min\{N_{1}^{3},(\kappa+N_{1})^{2}\},

which ultimately yields a contribution identical to that of Cases 1 and 2.
∎

In the case of the mKdV we have the following analogue:

Lemma 5.13
.

Let
|\varkappa|\geq\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\geq 1
and
f_{1},f_{2},f_{3}\in\mathcal{C}([-1,1];B_{\delta}\cap\mathscr{S})
satisfy

{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|f_{j}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}\lesssim{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}.

Then we have the estimates

(5.31)

\displaystyle\|(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi_{h}f_{1}\cdot\psi_{h}\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial})(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-3}\big[\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}-2s}+|\varkappa|^{-1-4s}\log|2\varkappa|\big]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},

(5.32)

\displaystyle\|(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi_{h}f_{1}\cdot\psi_{h}\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot\psi_{h}\tfrac{f_{3}}{2\varkappa+\partial})(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-5}\bigl[\kappa^{1-3s}+\bigl(1+\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{\varkappa^{2}}\bigr)|\varkappa|^{-6s}\log|2\varkappa|\bigr]\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

Proof.

The estimate (
5.31
) follows from the same argument used to prove (
5.30
); all that changes are the specific powers inside the sums.

Thus, it remains to consider the estimate (
5.32
). Proceeding as in the proof of (
5.30
), we may assume that
h=0
and bound

\mathrm{LHS}\eqref{L6I6-replacement mKdV}\lesssim\sum\limits_{N\geq 1}(|\varkappa|+N)^{-1}\log\big(4+\tfrac{N^{2}}{\varkappa^{2}}\big)\big\|P_{N}\bigl(\psi f_{1}\cdot\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot\psi\tfrac{f_{3}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr)\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}^{2}.

We then decompose further by frequency, using

\displaystyle\big\|P_{N}\big(\psi f_{1}\cdot\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot\psi\tfrac{f_{3}}{2\varkappa+p}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\biggl\|\sum\limits_{N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}\geq 1}\big\|P_{N}\big((\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{3}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\big)_{N_{3}}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}\biggr\|_{L^{2}_{t}}.

As everything is symmetric under the
N_{2}\leftrightarrow N_{3}
interchange, we may reduce matters to four possible cases:

Case 1:
\min\{N_{1},N\}\geq\max\{N_{2},N_{3}\}
.
Here we apply Bernstein’s inequality to bound

\displaystyle\big\|P_{N}\big((\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{3}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\big)_{N_{3}}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\|(\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}\|\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\|\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{3}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\big)_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim N_{1}^{-s-2}\min\{N_{1}^{2},\kappa+N_{1}\}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}-s}(|\varkappa|+N_{2})^{-1}N_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}-s}(|\varkappa|+N_{3})^{-1}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}.

Summing in
N_{2},N_{3}
and then in
N_{1}\approx N
using the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality we obtain a contribution to
\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{L6I6-replacement mKdV}
that is

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum\limits_{N\geq 1}|\varkappa|^{-4}(N\wedge|\varkappa|)^{2-4s}(|\varkappa|+N)^{-1}\log\big(4+\tfrac{N^{2}}{\varkappa^{2}}\big)N^{-2s-4}\min\{N^{4},(\kappa+N)^{2}\}\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-5}\bigl[\kappa^{1-3s}+\bigl(1+\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{\varkappa^{2}}\bigr)|\varkappa|^{-6s}\log|2\varkappa|\bigr]\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

Case 2:
\min\{N_{2},N\}\geq\max\{N_{1},N_{3}\}
.
A similar argument, this time placing
\psi\frac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}
in
L^{2}_{t,x}
yields the estimate

\displaystyle\big\|P_{N}\big((\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{3}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\big)_{N_{3}}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}-s}(|\varkappa|+N_{2})^{-1}N_{2}^{-s-2}\min\{N_{2}^{2},\kappa+N_{2}\}N_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}-s}(|\varkappa|+N_{3})^{-1}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}},

which yields a contribution to
\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{L6I6-replacement mKdV}
of

\lesssim\sum\limits_{N\geq 1}|\varkappa|^{-2}(N\wedge|\varkappa|)^{1-2s}(|\varkappa|+N)^{-3}\log\big(4+\tfrac{N^{2}}{\varkappa^{2}}\big)N^{-4s-3}\min\{N^{4},(\kappa+N)^{2}\}\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},

which yields an identical contribution to Case 1.

Case 3:
\min\{N_{1},N_{2}\}\geq\max\{N_{3},N\}
.
Here we apply Bernstein’s inequality at the output frequency and sum using the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality in
N_{1}\approx N_{2}
so that for fixed
N\geq 1
we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\sum\limits_{N_{1}\approx N_{2}\gtrsim N_{3},N}\big\|P_{N}\big((\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{3}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\big)_{N_{3}}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim N\left\|\sum\limits_{N_{1}\approx N_{2}\gtrsim N_{3},N}\|(\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{3}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\big)_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim N\sum\limits_{N_{1}\gtrsim N}N_{1}^{-4s-4}\min\{N_{1}^{4},(\kappa+N_{1})^{2}\}(|\varkappa|+N_{1})^{-2}|\varkappa|^{-2}(N_{1}\wedge|\varkappa|)^{1-2s}\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

We then obtain a contribution to
\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{L6I6-replacement mKdV}
of

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum\limits_{N\geq 1}\tfrac{N}{|\varkappa|+N}\log\big(4+\tfrac{N^{2}}{\varkappa^{2}}\big)\sum\limits_{N_{1}\gtrsim N}N_{1}^{-4s-4}\min\{N_{1}^{4},(\kappa+N_{1})^{2}\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times(|\varkappa|+N_{1})^{-2}|\varkappa|^{-2}(N_{1}\wedge|\varkappa|)^{1-2s}\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum\limits_{N_{1}\geq 1}|\varkappa|^{-3}N_{1}^{-4s-3}\min\{N_{1}^{4},(\kappa+N_{1})^{2}\}(|\varkappa|+N_{1})^{-2}(N_{1}\wedge|\varkappa|)^{1-2s}\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},

which gives an identical contribution to Cases 1 and 2.

Case 4:
\min\{N_{2},N_{3}\}\geq\max\{N_{1},N\}
.
Arguing as in Case 3, for fixed
N\geq 1
we may bound

\displaystyle\left\|\sum\limits_{N_{2}\approx N_{3}\gtrsim N_{1},N}\big\|P_{N}\big((\psi f_{1})_{N_{1}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big)_{N_{2}}\cdot\big(\psi\tfrac{f_{3}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\big)_{N_{3}}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim N\sum\limits_{N_{2}\gtrsim N}N_{2}^{-6s-3}\min\{N_{2}^{4},(\kappa+N_{2})^{2}\}(|\varkappa|+N_{2})^{-4}\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},

which yields a contribution to
\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{L6I6-replacement mKdV}
of

\sum\limits_{N_{2}\geq 1}|\varkappa|^{-1}N_{2}^{-6s-2}\min\{N_{2}^{4},(\kappa+N_{2})^{2}\}(|\varkappa|+N_{2})^{-4}\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

This gives an identical contribution to the previous cases.
∎

We are now in a position to prove our main error estimates for the NLS:

Lemma 5.14

(Error estimates for the NLS)
.

There exists
\delta>0
so that for all real
|\varkappa|\geq\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}}\geq 1
, Schwartz functions
q,f\in\mathcal{C}([-1,1];B_{\delta}\cap\mathscr{S})
satisfying

{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|f\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}\lesssim{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}},

and
\chi\in\{(\psi^{\ell})^{(j)}:6\leq\ell\leq 12,\,j=0,1\}
, we have the estimates

(5.33)

\displaystyle\left\|\int\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\,g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\pm\kappa)\,\chi_{h}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-1}\kappa^{-1-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2},

(5.34)

\displaystyle\left\|\int f\,\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\chi_{h}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-3}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{8s}{3}}+|\varkappa|^{-4s}\bigr]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2},

(5.35)

\displaystyle\left\|\gamma(\pm\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}\,\chi_{h}\right\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim\kappa^{-2-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2},

(5.36)

\displaystyle\left\|\int\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\,\big(g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\kappa)+g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(-\kappa)\big)\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\kappa^{-2-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2},

which are uniform in
\kappa
,
\varkappa
, and
h\in\mathbb{R}
. As ever,
\chi_{h}(x):=\chi(x-h)
.

Proof.

By translation invariance, it suffices to consider the case
h=0
.

Our basic technique here is to expand using the series (
3.19
), commute copies of
\psi
, and then use Hölder’s inequality in trace ideals. We first exhibit this technique to prove the auxiliary result (
5
) before turning our attention to the principal claims.

Given a test function
F\in\mathcal{C}([-1,1];\mathscr{S})
, using (
3.19
) we may write

\sgn(\varkappa)\int F\,g_{12}(\varkappa)^{[\geq 3]}\psi^{4}\,dx=\sum_{\ell=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{\ell-1}\tr\left\{\Lambda(\Gamma\Lambda)^{\ell}(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\psi^{4}F(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\}.

Applying Lemma
2.8
followed by the operator estimates (
3.7
) and (
2.25
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\tr\left\{\Lambda(\Gamma\Lambda)^{\ell}(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\psi^{4}F(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\}\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\|\Lambda\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{2}}^{2\ell-2}\|(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi q)(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{4}}^{3}\|(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi F)(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{4}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim C^{\ell}|\varkappa|^{-(2s+1)(\ell-1)-3}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{8s}{3}}+|\varkappa|^{-4s}\bigr]\delta^{2(\ell-1)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|F\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|F\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

where the implicit constant is independent of
\ell
. Summing in
\ell
and applying Young’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\Bigl\|\int F\,

\displaystyle g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\varkappa)\,\psi^{4}\,dx\Bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

(5.37)

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-3}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{8s}{3}}+|\varkappa|^{-4s}\bigr]\delta{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}\Big(\|F\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}+\delta{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|F\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}\Big).

The estimate (
5.33
) follows immediately from (
5
) by setting
\varkappa=\pm\kappa
,
F=\frac{\chi}{\psi^{4}}\frac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}
, and using (
2.14
) and (
2.8
) to bound
{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|F\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}
.

We turn now to (
5.34
) and recall that

(5.38)

\displaystyle\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}=\tfrac{1}{2}g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}-\tfrac{g_{12}\gamma}{2(2+\gamma)}.

By (
5
), the contribution of the first term to the left-hand side of (
5.34
) is easily seen to be acceptable. To estimate the contribution of the second term on the right-hand of (
5.38
), we take
f_{1}=\chi f/\psi^{4}
and
f_{2}=(2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}
and apply the estimate (
5.30
) to bound

\|(\varkappa\pm\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi^{2}f_{1}\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial})(\varkappa\pm\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{2}}\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-\frac{3}{2}}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{4s}{3}}+|\varkappa|^{-2s}\bigr]\delta{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}},

where we have used (
2.14
) with the estimates (
3.41
), (
3.43
) to bound

{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|f_{2}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}={\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}\lesssim{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}.

We then use (
3.19
) to write

\displaystyle\int\!\tfrac{\chi fg_{12}(\varkappa)}{2(2+\gamma(\varkappa))}\,\gamma\,dx=\sgn(\varkappa)\sum\limits_{\ell=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{\ell}\Bigl[

\displaystyle\tr\left\{(\Lambda\Gamma)^{\ell}(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi^{4}f_{1}\,\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial})(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle+{}

\displaystyle\tr\left\{(\Gamma\Lambda)^{\ell}(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi^{4}f_{1}\,\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial})(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\}\Bigr].

Repeating our basic technique using (
2.25
), we obtain

(5.39)

\displaystyle\left\|\int f_{1}\,\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\,\gamma(\varkappa)\,\psi^{4}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi q)(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{4}}^{2}\|(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi^{2}f_{1}\,\tfrac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial})(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-3}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{8s}{3}}+|\varkappa|^{-4s}\bigr]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2},

which completes the proof of (
5.34
). The estimate (
5.35
) follows analogously using (
2.25
) with
\varkappa=\kappa
:

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int F\,\gamma^{[\geq 4]}(\kappa)\,\chi\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\kappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi q)(\kappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{4}}^{4}\|(\kappa\pm\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\|\chi F\psi^{-4}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}

(5.40)

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-2-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2}\|F\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}.

Finally, we consider (
5.36
). Arguing in the same style, we bound

\displaystyle\left\|\int g_{12}^{[\geq 5]}(\kappa)\,\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\psi^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\Lambda\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{2}}\|(\kappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi q)(\kappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{4}}^{4}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\|(\kappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi^{8}\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial})(\kappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-2-\frac{11}{6}(2s+1)}\delta^{3}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2}\|\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-2-\frac{13}{6}(2s+1)}\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2},

where we have used that
|\varkappa|\geq\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}}
to estimate
\|\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{1}{3}(2s+1)}\delta
. For the remaining term we observe that integrating by parts we may write

\int g_{12}^{[3]}(\kappa)\,\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\,\psi^{12}\,dx=\int m[q,r,q,\psi^{12}\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}]\,dx,

where the symbol

m(\xi_{1},\xi_{2},\xi_{3},\xi_{4})=\tfrac{2}{(2\kappa+i\xi_{2})(2\kappa-i\xi_{3})(2\kappa+i\xi_{4})}

is a sum of terms in
\kappa^{5-|\beta|}S(\beta;2\kappa)
for
0\leq|\beta|\leq 5
, where at most one
\beta_{j}=2
. In particular, when considering the sum
g_{12}^{[3]}(\kappa)+g_{12}^{[3]}(-\kappa)
we see that the terms with even
|\beta|
cancel and hence

\int\Big(g_{12}^{[3]}(\kappa)+g_{12}^{[3]}(-\kappa)\Big)\,\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\,\psi^{12}\,dx=\int\widetilde{m}[q,r,q,\psi^{12}\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}]\,dx,

where
\widetilde{m}
has symbol given by a sum of terms in
\kappa^{5-|\beta|}S(\beta;2\kappa)
for
|\beta|=1,3,5
and at most one
\beta_{j}=2
. Applying the estimate (
5.25
) we then obtain

\left\|\int\Big(g_{12}^{[3]}(\kappa)+g_{12}^{[3]}(-\kappa)\Big)\,\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\,\psi^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\kappa^{-2-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2},

which completes the proof of (
5.36
).
∎

Similar arguments yield the following error estimates for the mKdV:

Lemma 5.15

(Error estimates for the mKdV)
.

There exists
\delta>0
so that for all real
|\varkappa|\geq\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\geq 1
,
q,f\in\mathcal{C}([-1,1];B_{\delta}\cap\mathscr{S})
satisfying

{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|f\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}\lesssim{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}},

and
\chi\in\{(\psi^{\ell})^{(j)}:6\leq\ell\leq 12,\,j=0,1,2\}
, we have the estimates

(5.41)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\biggl\|\int f\,g_{12}^{[3]}(\varkappa)\,\chi_{h}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}+\biggl\|\int f\,\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\chi_{h}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-3}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}-2s}+|\varkappa|^{-1-4s}\log^{4}\!|2\varkappa|\bigr]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},\end{aligned}

(5.42)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left\|\int f\,\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 5]}\,\chi_{h}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\\
&\qquad\qquad\quad\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-4-(s+\frac{1}{2})}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{5s}{2}}+|\varkappa|^{-\frac{1}{2}-5s}\log^{6}\!|2\varkappa|\bigr]\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},\end{aligned}

(5.43)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\biggl\|\int g_{21}^{[3]}(\pm\varkappa)\,\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\chi_{h}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-5}\bigl[\kappa^{1-2s}+|\varkappa|^{-4s}\bigr]\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},\end{aligned}

(5.44)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left\|\int\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\,\big(g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\kappa)+g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(-\kappa)\big)\,\chi_{h}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\lesssim\bigl[\kappa^{-1}+|\varkappa|^{-1}\bigr]\kappa^{-2-(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},\end{aligned}

(5.45)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left\|\int\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\,\big(g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\kappa)-g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(-\kappa)-\tfrac{1}{2\kappa^{3}}q^{2}r\big)\,\chi_{h}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{-3-(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},\end{aligned}

(5.46)

\displaystyle\left\|\gamma(\pm\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}\,\chi_{h}\right\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{5}{2}-(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},

(5.47)

\displaystyle\left\|\int\Big(\gamma(\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}-\gamma(-\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}\Big)\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\kappa^{-3-(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},

(5.48)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left\|\int\Big(\gamma(\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}+\gamma(-\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}-\tfrac{3}{2\kappa^{2}}qr\big(q\cdot\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}+\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\cdot r\big)\Big)\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\ \lesssim\kappa^{-3-(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},\end{aligned}

where the implicit constants are independent of
\kappa
,
\varkappa
, and
h\in\mathbb{R}
.

Proof.

The basic technique is that used to prove Lemma
5.14
; however, new cancellations need to be exhibited. We begin with the estimates on
\gamma
.

Mimicking (
5
), but using Lemma
2.7
with
p=4
yields (
5.46
). When taking
p=6
, we obtain instead

(5.49)

\displaystyle\bigl\|\gamma(\pm\kappa)^{[\geq 6]}\chi\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim[\kappa^{-5-3s}+\kappa^{-6-6s}\log(2\kappa)]\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-4-2s}\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

This estimate reduces (
5.47
) and (
5.48
) to consideration of the quartic terms, for which we turn to (
3.23
).
Evidently, every term in (
5.47
) and (
5.48
) can be written as a sum of paraproducts with symbols conforming to (
5.23
); however, by forming these particular linear combinations, we eliminate all terms with
|\beta|=0
. Thus we may apply Lemma
5.11
(with
\varkappa=\kappa
) and so deduce (
5.47
) and (
5.48
).

Applying our basic technique to
g_{12}
using Lemma
2.7
with
p=4
yields

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int f\,g_{12}^{[3]}(\varkappa)\,\chi_{h}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}+\biggl\|\int fg_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\varkappa)\,\chi\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\text{RHS\eqref{stack Dual mKdV}}.

Taking
p=5
and using also (
3.7
) yields

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int fg_{12}^{[\geq 5]}(\varkappa)\,\chi\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\text{RHS\eqref{stack Dual mKdV 2}},

These constitute a significant step toward proving (
5.41
) and (
5.42
). In view of (
5.38
), the proof of (
5.41
) is completed by the following:

(5.50)

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int f\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\gamma\,\chi\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-3}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}-2s}+|\varkappa|^{-1-4s}\log^{4}|2\varkappa|\bigr]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},

which is a consequence of the argument used in (
5.39
), but using Lemma
2.7
and (
5.31
) in place of their NLS analogues.

To prove (
5.42
), we use (
3.32
) and
\gamma^{[2]}=2g_{12}^{[1]}g_{21}^{[1]}
to rewrite (
3.40
) as

(5.51)

\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 5]}=\tfrac{1}{2}g_{12}^{[\geq 5]}-\tfrac{g_{12}(4+\gamma)}{4(2+\gamma)}\gamma^{[\geq 4]}+\tfrac{g_{12}g_{21}}{2+\gamma}g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}-\tfrac{1}{2}g_{12}^{[1]}g_{21}^{[1]}g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}+\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}g_{12}^{[1]}g_{21}^{[\geq 3]}.

The contribution of the first term was handled already.

Consider now the second term in (
5.51
). Applying Lemma
2.4
together with the estimates (
3.27
), (
3.35
), (
3.41
), and (
3.43
), we find that

F=\chi f\tfrac{g_{12}(4+\gamma)}{2(2+\gamma)\psi^{5}}\quad\text{satisfies}\quad{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|F\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}\delta{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}.

Thus, applying the basic technique and using Lemma
2.7
with
p=5
shows

\displaystyle\left\|\int\tfrac{g_{12}(4+\gamma)}{2(2+\gamma)}\gamma^{[\geq 4]}\,\chi\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\text{RHS\eqref{stack Dual mKdV 2}}.

The remaining three terms in (
5.51
) are handled in a parallel fashion, which we demonstrate using the first term. Set
F=f_{1}\,\frac{f_{2}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\,\frac{f_{3}}{2\varkappa+\partial}
with
f_{1}=\chi f/\psi^{6}
,
f_{2}=(2\varkappa-\partial)\frac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}
, and
f_{3}=(2\varkappa+\partial)g_{21}
. Then (
5.32
) implies

\displaystyle\|(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\psi^{3}

\displaystyle F(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-5}\bigl[\kappa^{1-3s}+\bigl(1+\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{\varkappa^{2}}\bigr)|\varkappa|^{-6s}\log|2\varkappa|\bigr]\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

Thus applying Lemma
2.7
with
p=6
, we find

\displaystyle\left\|\int F\,g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\varkappa)\,\psi^{6}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-5}\bigl[\kappa^{1-3s}+\bigl(1+\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{\varkappa^{2}}\bigr)|\varkappa|^{-6s}\log^{6}\!|2\varkappa|\bigr]\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},

which is no larger than RHS(
5.42
). This completes the proof of (
5.42
).

We turn now to (
5.43
). Combining (
5.42
) with (
3.21
) and Lemma
2.4
yields

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int g_{21}^{[3]}(\pm\varkappa)\,\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 5]}(\varkappa)\,\chi\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-1-(1+2s)}\cdot\text{RHS\eqref{stack Dual mKdV 2}}\lesssim\text{RHS\eqref{stack mKdV L2}}.

To continue, we employ (
3.38
). From Lemma
2.7
and (
2.20
), we find that

\displaystyle\biggl|\iint g_{21}^{[3]}\,F\psi^{3}\,dx\,dt\biggr|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\psi q(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{6}}^{3}\|(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}F(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-3}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{3s}{2}}+(1+\tfrac{\kappa}{|\varkappa|}\bigr)|\varkappa|^{-3s}\log^{3}\!|2\varkappa|\bigr]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}\|F\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

and consequently, that

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int g_{21}^{[3]}(\pm\varkappa)\,g_{12}^{[3]}(\varkappa)\,\chi\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl\|g_{21}^{[3]}(\pm\varkappa)\psi^{3}\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}^{2}\lesssim\text{RHS\eqref{stack mKdV L2}}.

On the other hand, using Lemma
2.7
, (
3.22
), and (
5.32
), we get

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int g_{21}^{[3]}(\pm\varkappa)\,g_{12}^{[1]}(\varkappa)\gamma^{[2]}(\varkappa)\,\chi\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\psi q)(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{6}}^{3}\|(\varkappa-\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\psi^{3}g_{12}^{[1]}(\varkappa)\gamma^{[2]}(\varkappa)(\varkappa+\partial)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}\mathfrak{I}_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-6}\bigl[\kappa^{1-3s}+\bigl(1+\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{\varkappa^{2}}\bigr)|\varkappa|^{-6s}\log^{6}\!|2\varkappa|\bigr]\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}\lesssim\text{RHS\eqref{stack mKdV L2}}.

This completes the proof of (
5.43
).

It remains to prove (
5.44
) and (
5.45
). We begin by reducing matters to the quartic terms. As
|\varkappa|\geq\sqrt{\kappa}
, so
\|\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}(s+\frac{1}{2})}\delta
. Thus we find

\left\|\int\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\cdot g_{12}^{[\geq 5]}(\pm\kappa)\,\chi\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{9}{2}-3s}\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},

by applying Lemma
2.7
with
p=5
.

Regarding the quartic terms, we observe that

\int\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}\cdot\Big(g_{12}^{[3]}(\kappa)-\tfrac{1}{4\kappa^{3}}q^{2}r\Big)\,\chi\,dx=\int m[q,r,q,\chi\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}]\,dx,

where the lowest order terms cancel to give a symbol
m
that is a sum of terms in
\kappa^{5-|\beta|}S(\beta;2\kappa)
for
1\leq|\beta|\leq 8
. Thus, (
5.27
) may be applied, which then yields (
5.44
). To obtain (
5.45
) we use (
5.28
) instead. This is possible due to the absence of any
|\beta|=1
terms in the multiplier.
∎

We are finally in a position to undertake the proof of Lemma
5.3
:

Proof of Lemma
5.3
.

We consider each of the currents in turn.

Proof of (
5.8
).

From Corollary
4.14
and (
4.14
),

j_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{[\geq 4]}=-i(2\varkappa+\partial)q\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}+i(2\varkappa-\partial)r\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}.

Writing

\psi_{h}^{6}(2\varkappa+\partial)q=2\varkappa(\psi_{h}^{6}q)-(\psi_{h}^{6})^{\prime}q+(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime}_{\leq\varkappa}+(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime}_{>\varkappa}

and invoking (
5.34
) and (
3.44
), we estimate

\displaystyle\left\|\int j_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{[\geq 4]}(\varkappa)\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa^{-2(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{2}+\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime}_{>\varkappa}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{-(s+\frac{3}{2})}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{s+\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa^{-2(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{2},

which completes the proof of (
5.8
).

Proof of (
5.9
).

From Corollary
4.14
, we compute

\displaystyle j_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{[\geq 4]}

\displaystyle=(4\varkappa^{2}+2\varkappa\partial+\partial^{2})q\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}-(4\varkappa^{2}-2\varkappa\partial+\partial^{2})r\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}

\displaystyle\quad-2q^{2}r\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}+2r^{2}q\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}.

Focusing on the first line in our expression for
j_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{[\geq 4]}
, we write

(5.52)

\displaystyle\psi_{h}^{3}

\displaystyle(4\varkappa^{2}+2\varkappa\partial+\partial^{2})q

\displaystyle=4\varkappa^{2}(\psi_{h}^{3}q)+2\varkappa(\psi_{h}^{3}q)^{\prime}+(\psi_{h}^{3}q)^{\prime\prime}-2\varkappa(\psi_{h}^{3})^{\prime}q+(\psi_{h}^{3})^{\prime\prime}q-2[(\psi_{h}^{3})^{\prime}q]^{\prime}.

Thus, using Bernstein’s inequality and (
3.44
), we estimate

\displaystyle\left\|\int P_{>\varkappa}\bigl[\psi_{h}^{3}(4\varkappa^{2}+2\varkappa\partial+\partial^{2})q\bigr]\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\psi_{h}^{9}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\varkappa^{-(1+2s)}\|\psi_{h}q\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{s+1}}\bigl\|\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\bigr\|_{X^{s+2}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-2(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2}.

On the other hand, an application of (
5.41
) yields

\displaystyle\left\|\int P_{\leq\varkappa}\bigl[\psi_{h}^{3}(4\varkappa^{2}+2\varkappa\partial+\partial^{2})q\bigr]\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\psi_{h}^{9}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\big[\varkappa^{-1}+\varkappa^{-2(2s+1)}\log^{4}\!|2\varkappa|\big]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2}.

We now demonstrate how to estimate the contribution of the final two terms in our expression for
j_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{[\geq 4]}
, using the former as our example. We first decompose into frequencies, as follows:

\left\|\int q^{2}r\,\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\sum\limits_{N_{j}\geq 1}\left\|\int(\psi_{h}^{3}q)_{N_{1}}(\psi_{h}^{3}r)_{N_{2}}(\psi_{h}^{3}q)_{N_{3}}\big(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)_{N_{4}}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}},

where the two highest frequencies must be comparable. By exploiting symmetries, we may reduce consideration to two cases, namely,
N_{1}\sim N_{2}\geq N_{3}\vee N_{4}
and
N_{1}\sim N_{4}\gtrsim N_{2}\geq N_{3}
.

To estimate the low frequencies we use

(5.53)

\displaystyle\|(\psi_{h}^{3}q)_{N}\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{\frac{1}{2}-s}\delta,

(5.54)

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)_{N}\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\varkappa+N)^{-1}N^{\frac{1}{2}-s}\delta,

which follow from Bernstein’s inequality and (
3.41
). To estimate the high frequencies we use

\displaystyle\|(\psi_{h}^{3}q)_{N}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{-1-s}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}},

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)_{N}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\varkappa+N)^{-1}N^{-1-s}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}},

which follow from Bernstein’s inequality and (
3.43
). Estimating the two lowest frequency terms in
L^{\infty}_{t,x}
and the two highest frequency terms in
L^{2}_{t,x}
we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\int q^{2}r\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\big[\varkappa^{-1}+\varkappa^{-2(2s+1)}\log|2\varkappa|\big]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2}.

This completes the proof of (
5.9
).

Proof of (
5.10
).

Recall that
\varkappa\in[\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}},\frac{1}{2}\kappa]\cup[2\kappa,\infty)
. We decompose

\displaystyle j_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{\mathrm{diff}}{}^{[\geq 4]}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{-i\big(1-\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\big)(2\varkappa+\partial)q\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}}_{\mathbf{err}_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{+i\big(1-\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\big)(2\varkappa-\partial)r\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}}_{\mathbf{err}_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{-\Big(\tfrac{2i\kappa^{3}}{\kappa-\varkappa}g_{12}(\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}+\tfrac{2i\kappa^{3}}{\kappa+\varkappa}g_{12}(-\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}\Big)\cdot\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}}_{\mathbf{err}_{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{-\Big(\tfrac{2i\kappa^{3}}{\kappa-\varkappa}g_{21}(\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}+\tfrac{2i\kappa^{3}}{\kappa+\varkappa}g_{21}(-\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}\Big)\cdot\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}}_{\mathbf{err}_{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{+\tfrac{i\kappa^{3}}{\kappa-\varkappa}\gamma(\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}+\tfrac{i\kappa^{3}}{\kappa+\varkappa}\gamma(-\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}}_{\mathbf{err}_{5}}

and note that by symmetry, it suffices to consider the contributions of the terms
\mathbf{err}_{j}
with
j=1,3,5
.

For
\mathbf{err}_{1}
, we first write

\big(1-\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\big)(2\varkappa+\partial)q=-\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\tfrac{2\varkappa+\partial}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}q-\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}q.

Using Lemma
2.8
together with (
3.44
), we estimate the contribution of the high frequencies as follows:

\displaystyle\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int\psi^{3}_{h}\tfrac{1}{2\kappa+\partial}\psi_{h}^{-3}\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{2\varkappa+\partial}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{>\varkappa}\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\psi_{h}^{9}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\bigl\|\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{2\varkappa+\partial}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{>\varkappa}\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-\frac{5}{2}-s}}\varkappa^{-(1+2s)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-2(1+2s)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2},

\displaystyle\left\|\int\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{1}{2\kappa+\partial}\psi_{h}^{-3}\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{2}}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{>\varkappa}\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\psi_{h}^{9}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\bigl\|\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{2}}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{>\varkappa}\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-\frac{5}{2}-s}}\varkappa^{-(1+2s)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-2(1+2s)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

The two low-frequency terms are estimated using Lemma
2.8
and (
5.34
):

\displaystyle\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{1}{2\kappa+\partial}\psi_{h}^{-3}\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{2\varkappa+\partial}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{\leq\varkappa}\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\psi_{h}^{9}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\lesssim\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\cdot\tfrac{\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}}\cdot|\varkappa|^{-3}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{8s}{3}}+|\varkappa|^{-4s}\bigr]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2}

and similarly,

\displaystyle\left\|\int\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{1}{2\kappa+\partial}\psi_{h}^{-3}\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{2}}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{\leq\varkappa}\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\psi_{h}^{9}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\lesssim\min\bigl\{\tfrac{\varkappa^{3}}{\kappa^{2}},\varkappa\bigr\}\cdot|\varkappa|^{-3}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{8s}{3}}+|\varkappa|^{-4s}\bigr]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

Collecting these estimates, we deduce that

\left\|\int\mathbf{err}_{1}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\bigl[\tfrac{\kappa}{\kappa+\varkappa}\kappa^{-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}+\varkappa^{-2(2s+1)}\bigr]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

To estimate the contribution of
\mathbf{err}_{3}
we define

f=(2\varkappa+\partial)\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big),

and apply the estimates (
3.41
) and (
3.43
) to see that

\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}\lesssim\delta\quad\text{and}\quad{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|f\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}\lesssim{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}.

We then write

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{3}

\displaystyle=-\tfrac{2i\kappa^{3}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\Big(g_{12}(\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}-g_{12}(-\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}\Big)\cdot\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{2i\kappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\Big(g_{12}(\kappa)^{{[\geq 3]}}+g_{12}(-\kappa)^{{[\geq 3]}}\Big)\cdot\tfrac{f}{2\varkappa+\partial}.

Applying the estimate (
5.33
) to the first term and the estimate (
5.36
) to the second, we obtain

\left\|\int\mathbf{err}_{3}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\tfrac{\kappa}{\kappa+\varkappa}\kappa^{-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

Finally, using (
5.35
) we estimate the contribution of
\mathbf{err}_{5}
by

\left\|\int\mathbf{err}_{5}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\tfrac{\kappa}{\kappa+\varkappa}\kappa^{-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}^{2},

which completes the proof of (
5.10
).

Proof of (
5.11
).

Recall that
\varkappa\in[\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}},\frac{1}{2}\kappa]\cup[2\kappa,\infty)
. We decompose

\displaystyle j_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{\mathrm{diff}\;[\geq 4]}=\underbrace{(1-\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}})(2\varkappa+\partial)q^{\prime}\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}}_{\mathbf{err}_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{+(1-\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}})(2\varkappa-\partial)r^{\prime}\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}}_{\mathbf{err}_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{+\big(\tfrac{4\kappa^{4}}{\kappa-\varkappa}g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\kappa)-\tfrac{4\kappa^{4}}{\kappa+\varkappa}g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(-\kappa)-\tfrac{2\kappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}q^{2}r\big)\cdot\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}}_{\mathbf{err}_{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{+\big(\tfrac{4\kappa^{4}}{\kappa-\varkappa}g_{21}^{[\geq 3]}(\kappa)-\tfrac{4\kappa^{4}}{\kappa+\varkappa}g_{21}^{[\geq 3]}(-\kappa)+\tfrac{2\kappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}r^{2}q\big)\cdot\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}}_{\mathbf{err}_{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{-\Big[\tfrac{2\kappa^{4}}{\kappa-\varkappa}\gamma^{[\geq 4]}(\kappa)-\tfrac{2\kappa^{4}}{\kappa+\varkappa}\gamma^{[\geq 4]}(-\kappa)-\tfrac{3\kappa^{2}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}qr\big(q\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}+r\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\big)\Big]}_{\mathbf{err}_{5}}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{+\tfrac{4\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}r\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 5]}+\tfrac{2\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}q^{2}r\cdot\bigl(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\bigr)^{[\geq 3]}}_{\mathbf{err}_{6}}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{-\tfrac{3\kappa^{2}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}q^{2}r\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}-\tfrac{\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}rg_{12}^{[1]}(\varkappa)\gamma^{[2]}(\varkappa)-\tfrac{2\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}qg_{21}^{[3]}(\varkappa)+\tfrac{\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}q^{2}rg_{21}^{[1]}(\varkappa)}_{\mathbf{err}_{7}}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{-\tfrac{4\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}q\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 5]}-\tfrac{2\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}r^{2}q\cdot\bigl(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\bigr)^{[\geq 3]}}_{\mathbf{err}_{8}}

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{-\tfrac{3\kappa^{2}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}r^{2}q\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}+\tfrac{\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}qg_{21}^{[1]}(\varkappa)\gamma^{[2]}(\varkappa)+\tfrac{2\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}r\cdot g_{12}^{[3]}(\varkappa)-\tfrac{\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}r^{2}qg_{12}^{[1]}(\varkappa)}_{\mathbf{err}_{9}}.

While the validity of this equality is, of course, elementary, the particular grouping of terms (and the addition of an extra term in
\mathbf{err}_{5}
that is then subtracted in
\mathbf{err}_{7}
and
\mathbf{err}_{9}
) represents a very delicate accounting for numerous cancellations.

As we will see, each term in this expansion individually yields an acceptable contribution to (
5.11
). We will treat
\mathbf{err}_{1},\mathbf{err}_{3},\mathbf{err}_{5},\mathbf{err}_{6}
, and
\mathbf{err}_{7}
in turn. The remaining terms are covered by this analysis and conjugation symmetry.

For
\mathbf{err}_{1}
, we first write

(1-\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}})(2\varkappa+\partial)q^{\prime}=-\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}q-\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{3}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}q.

Proceeding as in the proof of (
5.10
) and using (
3.44
), we estimate the contribution of the second term as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|\int\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{1}{2\kappa+\partial}\psi_{h}^{-3}\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{3}}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{>\varkappa}\,\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\psi_{h}^{9}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\bigl\|\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{3}}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{>\varkappa}\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-(3+s)}}\bigl\|\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\bigr\|_{X_{\kappa}^{2+s}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-2(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},

\displaystyle\left\|\int\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{1}{2\kappa+\partial}\psi_{h}^{-3}\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{3}}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{\leq\varkappa}\,\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\psi_{h}^{9}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\bigl\|\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{3}}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{\leq\varkappa}\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-(2+s)}}\bigl\|\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\bigr\|_{X_{\kappa}^{1+s}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-2(1+2s)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

To estimate the term with fewer derivatives, we write
\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}=\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 5]}+\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[3]}
. Arguing as above and using (
3.45
) in place of (
3.44
), we get

\displaystyle\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{1}{2\kappa+\partial}\psi_{h}^{-3}\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{>\varkappa}\,\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 5]}\,\psi_{h}^{9}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\bigl\|\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{>\varkappa}\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-(3+s)}}\bigl\|\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 5]}\bigr\|_{X_{\kappa}^{2+s}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\varkappa^{-3(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2},

while using (
5.42
), we estimate

\displaystyle\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{1}{2\kappa+\partial}\psi_{h}^{-3}\bigl(\psi_{h}^{3}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial}{2\kappa-\partial}q\bigr)_{\leq\varkappa}\,\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 5]}\,\psi_{h}^{9}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\tfrac{|\varkappa|^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{5s}{2}}+|\varkappa|^{-(\frac{1}{2}+5s)}\log^{6}\!|2\varkappa|\bigr]\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{jmKdVdiff-err}.

It remains to estimate the contribution of the quartic terms, which we expand using (
3.38
) and treat the two parts separately.

Setting
m_{1}=\frac{i\xi_{1}}{4\kappa^{2}+\xi_{1}^{2}}
and
m_{2}=\frac{-\xi_{1}^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}+\xi_{1}^{2}}
, and using (
3.21
) and (
3.22
), we have

\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,g_{21}^{[1]}(\varkappa)\gamma^{[2]}(\varkappa)=-\tfrac{4}{\varkappa}m_{1}\bigl[q,\tfrac{\varkappa\,r}{2\varkappa+\partial},\tfrac{\varkappa\,q}{2\varkappa-\partial},\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]-\tfrac{2}{\varkappa^{2}}m_{2}\bigl[q,\tfrac{\varkappa\,r}{2\varkappa+\partial},\tfrac{\varkappa\,q}{2\varkappa-\partial},\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr].

Applying both (
5.27
) and (
5.28
) from Lemma
5.11
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}q\,\big[g_{21}^{[1]}\gamma^{[2]}\big](\varkappa)\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{jmKdVdiff-err}.

For the remaining quartic term we first use (
3.13
) to write

\displaystyle\psi_{h}^{12}g_{21}^{[3]}(\varkappa)

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigl[r\gamma^{[2]}(\varkappa)\psi_{h}^{12}\bigr]+\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr[(\psi_{h}^{12})^{\prime}g_{21}^{[3]}(\varkappa)\bigr]

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigl[r\gamma^{[2]}(\varkappa)\psi_{h}^{12}\bigr]+\tfrac{1}{(2\varkappa+\partial)^{2}}\bigl[r\gamma^{[2]}(\varkappa)(\psi_{h}^{12})^{\prime}\bigr]+\tfrac{1}{(2\varkappa+\partial)^{2}}\bigr[(\psi_{h}^{12})^{\prime\prime}g_{21}^{[3]}(\varkappa)\bigr]

and so

\displaystyle\left\|\int\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,g_{21}^{[3]}(\varkappa)\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\int\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial q}{(2\varkappa-\partial)(4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2})}r\gamma^{[2]}(\varkappa)\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}+\left\|\int\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial q}{(2\varkappa-\partial)^{2}(4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2})}\,r\gamma^{[2]}(\varkappa)(\psi_{h}^{12})^{\prime}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\int\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial q}{(2\varkappa-\partial)^{2}(4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2})}\,g_{21}^{[3]}(\varkappa)(\psi_{h}^{12})^{\prime\prime}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}.

Using (
5.27
) again, we see that the contribution arising from the first two terms above is acceptable. For the last term, we estimate

\displaystyle\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial q}{(2\varkappa-\partial)^{2}(4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2})}\,g_{21}^{[3]}(\varkappa)(\psi_{h}^{12})^{\prime\prime}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\,\lesssim\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\bigl\|\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial}{(2\varkappa-\partial)^{2}(4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2})}q\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{-(1+s)}}\bigl\|g_{21}^{[3]}(\varkappa)\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{1+s}}\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-2(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

Collecting the estimates above, we obtain

\left\|\int\mathbf{err}_{1}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{jmKdVdiff-err}.

For
\mathbf{err}_{3}
we start by writing

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{3}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{4\kappa^{5}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\big(g_{12}(\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}-g_{12}(-\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}-\tfrac{1}{2\kappa^{3}}q^{2}r\big)\,\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{4\kappa^{4}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\big(g_{12}(\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}+g_{12}(-\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}\big)\,\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}.

We then apply the estimates (
5.44
) and (
5.45
) with
f=(2\varkappa+\partial)\frac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}
together with (
3.41
) and (
3.43
) to bound

\left\|\int\mathbf{err}_{3}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\tfrac{\kappa}{\kappa+\varkappa}\kappa^{-(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

For
\mathbf{err}_{5}
we may write

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{5}

\displaystyle=-\tfrac{2\kappa^{5}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\Big(\gamma(\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}-\gamma(-\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{2\kappa^{4}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\Big(\gamma(\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}+\gamma(-\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}-\tfrac{3}{2\kappa^{2}}qr\big(q\cdot\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}+r\cdot\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\big)\Big)

and then use (
5.47
) and (
5.48
) to bound

\left\|\int\mathbf{err}_{5}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\tfrac{\kappa}{\kappa+\varkappa}\kappa^{-(2s+1)}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}.

For
\mathbf{err}_{6}
we first apply the estimate (
5.42
) to bound

\displaystyle\left\|\int\tfrac{4\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}r\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 5]}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\tfrac{\varkappa^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{5s}{2}}+\varkappa^{-(\frac{1}{2}+5s)}\log^{6}|2\varkappa|\bigr]\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{jmKdVdiff-err}.

Next, we use (
3.20
) and
[\psi_{h}^{12},\tfrac{\partial}{2\varkappa-\partial}]=-\frac{2\varkappa}{2\varkappa-\partial}(\psi_{h}^{12})^{\prime}\frac{1}{2\varkappa-\partial}
to write

\displaystyle\psi_{h}^{12}\,q^{2}r

\displaystyle=\psi_{h}^{12}\,4\varkappa^{3}g_{12}^{[3]}(\varkappa)+\tfrac{4\varkappa^{3}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\bigl[(\psi_{h}^{12})^{\prime}g_{12}^{[3]}(\varkappa)\bigr]

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{\partial}{2\varkappa-\partial}\bigl[\psi_{h}^{12}q\tfrac{2\varkappa r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\tfrac{2\varkappa q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\bigr]+\psi_{h}^{12}\,q\tfrac{r^{\prime}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\tfrac{2\varkappa q}{2\varkappa-\partial}-\psi_{h}^{12}\,qr\tfrac{q^{\prime}}{2\varkappa-\partial}.

From Corollary
3.5
and elementary manipulations, we have

{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa+\partial}\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-3-2s}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}.

Thus, by taking
m_{1}(\xi)=\frac{i\xi_{2}}{2\kappa+i\xi_{2}}
,
m_{2}(\xi)=\frac{i\xi_{3}}{2\kappa-i\xi_{3}}
, and applying (
5.43
) to the first term, (
5.41
) to the second, and (
5.27
), (
3.42
), (
3.44
) to the remaining terms, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\int\tfrac{2\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}q^{2}r\,\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\tfrac{\varkappa^{5}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int g_{12}^{[3]}(\varkappa)\,\big(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\tfrac{\varkappa^{5}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int(\psi_{h}^{12})^{\prime}g_{12}^{[3]}(\varkappa)\cdot\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigl(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\bigr)^{[\geq 3]}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\tfrac{\varkappa^{3}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int m_{1}\Bigl[q,\tfrac{2\kappa+\partial}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigl(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\bigr)^{[\geq 3]},\tfrac{2\varkappa q}{2\varkappa-\partial},\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\Bigr]\,\psi^{12}_{h}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\tfrac{\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int m_{1}\Bigl[q,\tfrac{2\kappa+\partial}{2\varkappa+\partial}r,\tfrac{2\varkappa q}{2\varkappa-\partial},\bigl(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\bigr)^{[\geq 3]}\Bigr]\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\tfrac{\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int m_{2}\Bigl[q,r,\tfrac{2\kappa-\partial}{2\varkappa-\partial}q,\bigl(\tfrac{g_{21}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\bigr)^{[\geq 3]}\Bigr]\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{jmKdVdiff-err}.

For
\mathbf{err}_{7}
we first observe that

\displaystyle-\tfrac{2\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\int qg_{21}^{[3]}(\varkappa)\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx

\displaystyle\qquad=\tfrac{4\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\int\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\,r\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx-\tfrac{2\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\int\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}g_{21}^{[3]}(\varkappa)\,(\psi_{h}^{12})^{\prime}\,dx.

Applying (
5.41
), the second integral contributes a constant multiple of

\tfrac{1}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\bigl[\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}-2s}+\varkappa^{-(1+4s)}\log^{4}|2\varkappa|\bigr]\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}^{2}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{jmKdVdiff-err}.

Thus the remaining quartic terms are

\displaystyle-\tfrac{3\kappa^{2}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}q^{2}r\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}+\tfrac{2\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\,r+\tfrac{\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}q^{2}r\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}.

A quick computation shows that

\displaystyle-\tfrac{3\kappa^{2}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}q^{2}r\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}+\tfrac{\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}q^{2}r\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}

\displaystyle\qquad=-\tfrac{2\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}q^{2}\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}-\tfrac{6\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}m_{1}\bigl[q,\tfrac{2\kappa+\partial}{2\varkappa+\partial}r,q,\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]

\displaystyle\qquad\quad-\tfrac{3\kappa^{2}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}m_{2}\bigl[q,\tfrac{r^{\prime}}{2\varkappa+\partial},q,\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]-\tfrac{\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}m_{3}\bigl[q,r,q,\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr],

where
m_{1}(\xi)=\frac{i\xi_{2}}{2\kappa+i\xi_{2}}\frac{1}{4\kappa^{2}+\xi_{4}^{2}}
,
m_{2}(\xi)=\frac{i\xi_{4}}{4\kappa^{2}+\xi_{4}^{2}}
and
m_{3}(\xi)=\frac{(i\xi_{2})^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}
. To continue, we observe that

\displaystyle-\tfrac{2\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}q^{2}\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}+\tfrac{2\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\,r

\displaystyle\qquad=\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}m_{4}\bigl[\tfrac{2\kappa-\partial}{2\varkappa-\partial}q,r,\tfrac{2\varkappa q}{2\varkappa-\partial},\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]-\tfrac{2\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}m_{4}\bigl[\tfrac{2\kappa-\partial}{2\varkappa-\partial}q,r,\tfrac{q^{\prime}}{2\varkappa-\partial},\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]

\displaystyle\qquad\quad-\tfrac{\varkappa^{2}}{2(\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2})}m_{3}\bigl[\tfrac{2\varkappa q}{2\varkappa-\partial},r,\tfrac{2\varkappa q}{2\varkappa-\partial},\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr],

where
m_{4}(\xi)=\frac{i\xi_{1}}{2\kappa-i\xi_{1}}\frac{1}{4\kappa^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}
. Applying the estimate (
5.27
), we obtain

\displaystyle\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int m_{1}\bigl[q,\tfrac{2\kappa+\partial}{2\varkappa+\partial}r,q,\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int m_{2}\bigl[q,\tfrac{r^{\prime}}{2\varkappa+\partial},q,\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\tfrac{\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int m_{3}\bigl[q,r,q,\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\tfrac{\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int m_{3}\bigl[\tfrac{\varkappa q}{2\varkappa-\partial},r,\tfrac{\varkappa q}{2\varkappa-\partial},\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int m_{4}\bigl[\tfrac{2\kappa-\partial}{2\varkappa-\partial}q,r,\tfrac{\varkappa q}{2\varkappa-\partial},\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\tfrac{\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2}}\left\|\int m_{4}\bigl[\tfrac{2\kappa-\partial}{2\varkappa-\partial}q,r,\tfrac{q^{\prime}}{2\varkappa-\partial},\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{jmKdVdiff-err}.

Collecting all our bounds, we obtain the estimate (
5.11
).
∎

6.
Tightness

Let
\chi\in\mathcal{C}^{\infty}_{c}
be an even non-negative function supported in
\{|x|\leq 1\}
with
\|\chi\|_{L^{1}}=1
and define

\phi(x)=\int_{0}^{|x|}\chi(y-2)\,dy.

For
R\geq 1
we define the rescaled function
\phi_{R}(x)=\phi(\frac{x}{R})
. Notice that
\phi_{R}
plays the role of a smooth cut-off to large
|x|
and so leads naturally to the following formulation of tightness:

Definition 6.1
.

A bounded subset
Q\subset H^{s}
is
tight
in
H^{s}
if

\phi_{R}q\rightarrow 0\text{ in }H^{s}\text{ as }R\rightarrow\infty,\text{ uniformly for }q\in Q.

We first prove that tightness of
q
implies tightness of
g_{12}
:

Lemma 6.2
.

For
\delta>0
sufficiently small,

(6.1)

\displaystyle\|\phi_{R}g_{12}\|_{H^{s+1}_{\varkappa}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\phi_{R}q\|_{H^{s}_{\varkappa}}+(|\varkappa|R)^{-1}\|q\|_{H^{s}_{\varkappa}},

(6.2)

\displaystyle\|\phi_{R}g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}\|_{H^{s+1}_{\varkappa}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-(2s+1)}\delta^{2}\Big(\|\phi_{R}q\|_{H^{s}_{\varkappa}}+(|\varkappa|R)^{-1}\|q\|_{H^{s}_{\varkappa}}\Big),

(6.3)

\displaystyle\|\phi_{R}\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\|_{H^{s+1}_{\varkappa}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varkappa|^{-(2s+1)}\delta^{2}\Big(\|\phi_{R}q\|_{H^{s}_{\varkappa}}+(|\varkappa|R)^{-1}\|q\|_{H^{s}_{\varkappa}}\Big).

uniformly for
|\varkappa|\geq 1
,
R\geq 1
