, and
q\in B_{\delta}
. Here
g_{12}=g_{12}(\varkappa)
and
\gamma=\gamma(\varkappa)
.

Proof.

Using the identity (
3.12
) we write

\phi_{R}g_{12}=-\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big(\phi_{R}q(1+\gamma)\big)-\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big(\phi_{R}^{\prime}g_{12}\big),

so the estimate (
6.1
) follows from the estimates (
3.24
) and (
3.27
).

Similarly, the estimate (
6.2
) follows from the identity

\phi_{R}g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}=-\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big(\phi_{R}q\gamma\big)-\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa-\partial}\big(\phi_{R}^{\prime}g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}\big)

and the estimates (
3.25
) and (
3.27
). The estimate (
6.3
) is then a corollary of the estimates (
6.1
), (
6.2
), (
2.5
), and (
3.39
).
∎

We will prove tightness for solutions of (
NLS
) and (
mKdV
) by considering the equation satisfied by
\operatorname{Re}\rho(\varkappa)
. Our next lemma shows that this is a suitable quantity to consider. The utility of this density should not be conflated with that of the currents used to prove the local smoothing effect. In particular, in the (
NLS
) setting, it is the
imaginary
part of
\rho
that is used to prove local smoothing.

Lemma 6.3
.

For
\delta
sufficiently small we have

(6.4)

\|\phi_{R}q\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\approx\int_{1}^{\kappa}\varkappa^{2s+1}\Big(\pm\operatorname{Re}\int\rho\,\phi_{R}^{2}\,dx\Big)\,\tfrac{d\varkappa}{\varkappa}+\mathcal{O}\Big(R^{-2}\|q\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|q\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}^{2}\Big),

uniformly for
q\in B_{\delta}\cap\mathscr{S}
,
R\geq 1
, and
\kappa\geq 1
.

Proof.

As in the proof of Lemma
5.1
, we write

\rho^{[2]}=\mathbf{R}[q,r]=\tfrac{1}{2}\big(q\cdot\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}+\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot r\big)

and compute that

\operatorname{Re}\int\mathbf{R}[\phi_{R}q,\phi_{R}r]\,dx=\pm 2\varkappa\|\phi_{R}q\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}.

Applying Lemma
2.1
we then obtain

\|\phi_{R}q\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\approx\int_{1}^{\kappa}\varkappa^{2s+1}\Big(\pm\operatorname{Re}\int\mathbf{R}[\phi_{R}q,\phi_{R}r]\,dx\Big)\,\tfrac{d\varkappa}{\varkappa}+\mathcal{O}\Big(\|q\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}^{2}\Big).

It remains to bound the contribution of the difference

\displaystyle\int\rho\,\phi_{R}^{2}\,dx-\int\mathbf{R}[\phi_{R}q,\phi_{R}r]\,dx

\displaystyle=\int\Big(\mathbf{R}[q,r]\,\phi_{R}^{2}-\mathbf{R}[\phi_{R}q,\phi_{R}r]\Big)\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\int\Big(q\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}-r\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\Big)\,\phi_{R}^{2}\,dx.

For the first term we bound

\displaystyle\left|\int\Big(\mathbf{R}[q,r]\,\phi_{R}^{2}-\mathbf{R}[\phi_{R}q,\phi_{R}r]\Big)\,dx\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\phi_{R}q\|_{H_{\varkappa}^{s}}\bigl\|\bigl[\phi_{R},\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa-\partial}\bigr]q\bigr\|_{H_{\varkappa}^{-s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa^{-(1+2s)}(\varkappa R)^{-1}\|\phi_{R}q\|_{H^{s}_{\varkappa}}\|q\|_{H^{s}_{\varkappa}}.

For the second term we apply the estimate (
6.3
) and Young’s inequality to bound

\displaystyle\left|\int q\,\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\phi_{R}^{2}\,dx\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa^{-(2s+1)}\|\phi_{R}q\|_{H^{s}_{\varkappa}}\|\phi_{R}\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\|_{H^{s+1}_{\varkappa}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa^{-2(2s+1)}\delta^{2}\|\phi_{R}q\|_{H^{s}_{\varkappa}}^{2}+\varkappa^{-2(2s+1)}(\varkappa R)^{-2}\delta^{2}\|q\|_{H^{s}_{\varkappa}}^{2}.

As a consequence, we may integrate to obtain

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{\kappa}\varkappa^{2s+1}\left|\int\rho\,\phi_{R}^{2}\,dx-\operatorname{Re}\int\mathbf{R}[\phi_{R}q,\phi_{R}r]\,dx\right|\,\tfrac{d\varkappa}{\varkappa}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\lesssim R^{-1}\|\phi_{R}q\|_{H^{s}}\|q\|_{H^{s}}+\delta^{2}\|\phi_{R}q\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\delta^{2}R^{-2}\|q\|^{2}_{H^{s}},

from which we derive the estimate (
6.4
) by taking
\delta
sufficiently small.
∎

We now arrive at the center-piece of this section:

Proposition 6.4

(Tightness of the flows)
.

For
\delta>0
sufficiently small, the following holds: If
Q\subset B_{\delta}\cap\mathscr{S}
is tight and equicontinuous in
H^{s}
then

\Big\{q(t)=e^{tJ\nabla H_{\star}}q:q\in Q,\;t\in[-1,1]\Big\}\quad\text{is tight in $H^{s}$}.

Here
\star=\mathrm{NLS},\mathrm{mKdV}
.

We will prove this result for each of the two flows separately. One element common to both is the following: For
\sigma=s+\frac{1}{2}
or
\sigma=s+1
, we have

(6.5)

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{6}(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}F\|_{L_{h}^{1}L_{t}^{2}H^{\sigma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\psi_{h}^{3}(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\|_{L^{1}_{h}H^{1}}\|\psi_{h}^{3}F\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}L^{2}_{t}H^{\sigma}}\lesssim\|F\|_{X^{\sigma}}.

Proof of Proposition
6.4
for (
NLS
).

Taking
t\in[-1,1]
and
R\geq 1
, we multiply the equation (
4.37
) by
\phi_{R}^{2}
, take the real part, and integrate by parts to obtain

(6.6)

\operatorname{Re}\int\bigl[\rho(t)-\rho(0)\bigr]\,\phi_{R}^{2}\,dx=\operatorname{Re}\int_{0}^{t}\int j_{\mathrm{NLS}}\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\,d\tau.

Choosing
\kappa\geq 1
and applying the estimate (
6.4
) and the a priori estimate (
4.12
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\phi_{R}q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\phi_{R}q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+R^{-2}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{1}^{\kappa}\varkappa^{2s+1}\biggl\|\int j_{\mathrm{NLS}}\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\,\tfrac{d\varkappa}{\varkappa}.

Integrating by parts and using (
3.11
) we may write

\displaystyle\int j_{\mathrm{NLS}}\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx

\displaystyle=-i\int q\,\Big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-\tfrac{1}{2}r\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+i\int r\,\Big((2\varkappa+\partial)\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}+\tfrac{1}{2}q\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{i}{2}\int\log[2+\gamma]\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime\prime\prime}\,dx.

For the final term we may apply (
3.28
) and (
4.12
) to obtain

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\log[2+\gamma]\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime\prime\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim R^{-2}.

The remaining two terms are treated identically, so it suffices to consider the first. We decompose

(6.7)

\displaystyle q=\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}-\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}

and estimate the contribution of the low frequency term via

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\Big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-\tfrac{1}{2}r\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\bigl\|\tfrac{\kappa^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{-s}}\bigl\|(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-\tfrac{1}{2}r\big)\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{-2s}\|q\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s}}\bigl\|(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\bigr\|_{H^{1}}\Big(\|(2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s}}+\|q\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{2}}\kappa^{-2s}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}.

To continue, we use (
2.3
) to express the high frequency term via

\displaystyle\int\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\Big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-\tfrac{1}{2}r\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx

\displaystyle\qquad=\tfrac{7}{512}\iint\Bigl(\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]q+\tfrac{\partial^{2}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\Bigr)\,\psi_{h}^{6}\,\Big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-\tfrac{1}{2}r\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\,dh.

For the commutator term, we apply the local smoothing estimates (
3.43
) and (
5.12
), together with (
2.11
) and (
6.5
) to bound

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]q\,\psi_{h}^{6}\,\Big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-\tfrac{1}{2}r\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t,h}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\bigl\|\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]q\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,h}H^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-\tfrac{1}{2}r\big)\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{h}L_{t}^{2}H^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{-(2s+\frac{3}{2})}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}\Big(\bigl\|(2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\bigr\|_{X^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}+\|q\|_{X^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{-(2s+\frac{3}{2})}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}.

For the remaining term, we use (
3.43
), (
5.12
), (
5.13
), and (
6.5
), as follows:

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\tfrac{\partial^{2}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\psi_{h}^{6}\,\Big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-\tfrac{1}{2}r\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t,h}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\bigl\|\tfrac{\partial^{2}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{h}^{\infty}L^{2}_{t}H^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-\tfrac{1}{2}r\big)\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{h}L_{t}^{2}H^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{-(2s+1)}\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime}\|_{L_{h}^{\infty}L^{2}_{t}H_{\kappa}^{s-\frac{1}{2}}}\Big(\bigl\|(2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\bigr\|_{X^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}+\|q\|_{X^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{-(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}+\kappa^{-\frac{3}{2}(2s+1)}\delta\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}.

Combining these bounds we see that for any
\kappa\geq 1
we have the estimate

\displaystyle\|\phi_{R}q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\phi_{R}q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}^{2}+\delta\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa^{2s+1}R^{-2}+\bigl(\kappa R^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\kappa^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}\bigr)\delta^{2}.

Taking the supremum over
q(0)\in Q
and using that
Q
is tight we obtain

\displaystyle\limsup_{R\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{q(0)\in Q}\|\phi_{R}q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup\limits_{q(0)\in Q}\delta\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}+\kappa^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}\delta^{2}.

Using that
Q
is equicontinuous, the result follows by sending
\kappa\rightarrow\infty
.
∎

Proof of Proposition
6.4
for (
mKdV
).

Mimicking the argument given in the (
NLS
) case reduces matters to proving a suitable
L^{1}_{t}
estimate for

(6.8)

\displaystyle\int

\displaystyle j_{\mathrm{mKdV}}\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx

\displaystyle=\int q^{\prime}\,\Big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-r\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx+\int r^{\prime}\,\Big((2\varkappa+\partial)\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}+q\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\int q\,\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{\prime}(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime\prime}\,dx-\int r\,\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{\prime}(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime\prime}\,dx-2\varkappa\int qr\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-2\int\rho\,qr\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx+\int\rho\,(4\varkappa^{2}+\partial^{2})(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx.

From Corollary
3.5
, (
2.16
), (
4.12
), and (
5.15
) we have

(6.9)

\displaystyle\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}+\|\rho\|_{X^{s+1}}\lesssim\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}.

Thus, we may estimate the final term in (
6.8
) as follows:

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\rho\,(4\varkappa^{2}+\partial^{2})(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\big(\varkappa^{2}R^{-\frac{1}{2}}+R^{-\frac{5}{2}}\big)\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}.

To estimate the contribution of the remaining terms, we rely on the decomposition (
6.7
). We first bound the low-frequency contribution to each of the terms in (
6.8
), before treating the high-frequency terms. From (
3.41
) and (
4.12
), we have

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}q^{\prime}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\Big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-r\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\bigl\|\tfrac{\kappa^{2}q^{\prime}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{-s}}\bigl\|(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\bigr\|_{H^{1}}\bigl\|(2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-r\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s}}\lesssim\kappa^{1-2s}R^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}.

Arguing similarly, we also obtain

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{\prime}\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-2s}R^{-\frac{3}{2}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2},

\displaystyle\varkappa\biggl\|\int\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,r\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa\kappa^{-2s}R^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}.

For the penultimate term in (
6.8
), we decompose both
q
and
r
according to (
6.7
):

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\rho(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl\|\tfrac{\kappa^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}^{2}\bigl\|\rho\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}\bigl\|(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\bigr\|_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}R^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{4}.

To estimate the contribution of the high-frequency term in the decomposition (
6.7
), we use (
2.3
). For example, we write

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\tfrac{\partial^{3}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\Big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-\tfrac{1}{2}r\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\biggl\|\int\Bigl(\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{3}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]q+\tfrac{\partial^{3}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\Bigr)\,\psi_{h}^{6}\,\Big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-\tfrac{1}{2}r\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t,h}}.

Using (
3.43
), (
5.15
), (
6.5
), and (
2.11
), we get

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{3}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]q\,\psi_{h}^{6}\,\Big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-r\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t,h}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\bigl\|\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{3}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]q\bigr\|_{L_{h}^{\infty}L_{t}^{\infty}H^{-(s+1)}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-\tfrac{1}{2}r\big)\bigr\|_{L_{h}^{1}L_{t}^{2}H^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{-(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}.

Using also (
5.16
), we estimate

\displaystyle\left|\int\int_{0}^{t}\int\tfrac{\partial^{3}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\psi_{h}^{6}\,\Big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-r\Big)\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\,d\tau\,dh\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\bigl\|\tfrac{\partial^{3}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{h}^{\infty}L_{t}^{2}H^{-(s+1)}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\big((2\varkappa-\partial)\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-r\big)\bigr\|_{L_{h}^{1}L_{t}^{2}H^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{-(2s+1)}\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime}\|_{L_{h}^{\infty}L^{2}_{t}H_{\kappa}^{s}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{-(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}+\kappa^{-\frac{3}{2}(1+2s)}\log^{2}(2\kappa)\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Arguing similarly, we also obtain

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]q\,\psi_{h}^{6}\,\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{\prime}\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t,h}}\lesssim R^{-1}\kappa^{-2(1+s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}},

\displaystyle\varkappa\biggl\|\int\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]q\,\psi_{h}^{6}\,r(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t,h}}\lesssim\varkappa\kappa^{-2(1+s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}

and

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\tfrac{\partial^{2}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\psi_{h}^{6}\,\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{\prime}\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t,h}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim R^{-1}\kappa^{-2(1+s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}+R^{-1}\kappa^{-(\frac{5}{2}+3s)}\log^{2}(2\kappa)\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3},

\displaystyle\varkappa\biggl\|\int\int_{0}^{t}\int\tfrac{\partial^{2}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\psi_{h}^{6}\,r(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t,h}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\varkappa\kappa^{-2(1+s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}+\varkappa\kappa^{-(\frac{5}{2}+3s)}\log^{2}(2\kappa)\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

This leaves us to handle the high-frequency contribution to the penultimate term in (
6.8
), which involves the combination

\displaystyle\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\cdot\Bigl(\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]r+\tfrac{\partial^{2}(\psi_{h}^{6}r)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\Bigr)\psi_{h}^{6}+\Bigl(\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]q+\tfrac{\partial^{2}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\Bigr)\cdot r\,\psi_{h}^{6}.

We illustrate the estimation of these contributions using the latter summand. Using (
2.11
), (
6.5
), and (
6.9
), we get

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]q\cdot r\,\psi_{h}^{6}\,\rho\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t,h}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\bigl\|\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]q\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,h}H^{-s}}\|r\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\rho\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{h}L_{t}^{2}H^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3},

\displaystyle\biggl\|\int\tfrac{\partial^{2}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,r\,\rho\,\psi_{h}^{6}\,(\phi_{R}^{2})^{\prime}\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t,h}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\bigl\|\tfrac{\partial^{2}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{h}^{\infty}L_{t}^{2}H^{-s}}\|q\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s}}\|\rho\|_{X^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}_{\kappa}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}+\kappa^{-\frac{3}{2}(1+2s)}\log^{2}(2\kappa)\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{5}.

The proof may now be completed exactly as in the NLS case.
∎

7.
Convergence of the difference flows

Our main goal in this section is to prove the following:

Proposition 7.1

(Difference flow approximates the identity)
.

Let
\delta>0
be sufficiently small and fix
\star\in\{\mathrm{NLS},\mathrm{mKdV}\}
. Given
Q\subset B_{\delta}\cap\mathscr{S}
that is equicontinuous in
H^{s}
and
\varkappa\geq 4
, we have

\psi_{h}^{12}g_{12}\bigl(\varkappa;e^{tJ\nabla(H_{\star}-H_{\star}^{\kappa})}q\bigr)\rightarrow\psi_{h}^{12}g_{12}(\varkappa;q)\text{ in }\mathcal{C}([-1,1];H^{s+1})\text{ as }\kappa\rightarrow\infty,

uniformly for
q\in Q
and
h\in\mathbb{R}
.

Proof for (
NLS-diff
).

Applying Proposition
4.6
we see that

Q^{*}=\{e^{tJ\nabla(H_{\mathrm{NLS}}-H_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{\kappa})}q:q\in Q,\,t\in\mathbb{R}\}

is equicontinuous in
H^{s}
. By Proposition
3.2
, for any
\varkappa\geq 1
, the map
q\mapsto g_{12}(\varkappa)
is a diffeomorphism from
B_{\delta}\rightarrow H^{s+1}
; moreover, this map commutes with spatial translations. Thus the set

\{g_{12}(\varkappa;q):q\in Q^{*}\}\quad\text{and so also}\quad\{\psi_{h}^{12}g_{12}(\varkappa;q):q\in Q^{*},\,h\in\mathbb{R}\}

is equicontinuous in
H^{s+1}
. As a consequence, it suffices to show that

(7.1)

\lim\limits_{\kappa\rightarrow\infty}\sup\limits_{q\in Q}\sup\limits_{h\in\mathbb{R}}\left\|i\frac{d}{dt}\left(\psi_{h}^{12}g_{12}(\varkappa;e^{tJ\nabla(H_{\mathrm{NLS}}-H_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{\kappa})}q)\right)\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}=0.

Using the identities (
3.12
) for
g_{12}
and (
3.11
) for
\gamma
we may write

-g_{12}(\varkappa)^{\prime\prime}+2qrg_{12}(\varkappa)+2q^{2}g_{21}(\varkappa)=-4\varkappa^{2}g_{12}(\varkappa)-[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\,(2\varkappa+\partial)q.

Thus, we may rewrite (
4.35
) as

i\frac{d}{dt}g_{12}(\varkappa)=\sum\limits_{j=1}^{11}\mathbf{err}_{j},

where we define

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{1}=\tfrac{4\varkappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}g_{12}(\varkappa),\qquad\mathbf{err}_{2}=\tfrac{4\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{3}=[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}},\qquad\mathbf{err}_{4}=-\tfrac{32\kappa^{4}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}g_{12}(\varkappa)\,\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{5}=\tfrac{16\kappa^{4}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}g_{12}(\varkappa)\Bigl[\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{\partial r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}-\tfrac{\partial q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{6}=\bigl[\tfrac{8\kappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}+16\kappa^{2}\bigr]g_{12}(\varkappa)\,\tfrac{\partial q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{\partial r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{7}=-8\kappa^{2}g_{12}(\varkappa)\partial^{2}\Bigl[\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{8}=2g_{12}(\varkappa)\,\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{\partial^{2}r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{9}=\tfrac{2\kappa^{3}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\,\Bigl[-g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\kappa)+g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(-\kappa)\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{10}=-\tfrac{2\kappa^{4}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\,\Bigl[g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\kappa)+g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(-\kappa)\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{11}=g_{12}(\varkappa)\Bigl[\tfrac{2\kappa^{3}}{\kappa-\varkappa}\gamma(\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}+\tfrac{2\kappa^{3}}{\kappa+\varkappa}\gamma(-\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}\Bigr].

It remains to bound each of the terms
\mathbf{err}_{j}
. We will rely on the a priori estimate (
4.12
) and the local smoothing estimate (
5.18
), which yield

(7.2)

\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}=\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}+\|q\|_{X^{s+\frac{1}{2}}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

We will also employ the estimates recorded in Corollary
5.7
, as well as the bounds

(7.3)

\displaystyle\|q\|_{X^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{2}{3}(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}},

(7.4)

\displaystyle\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{X^{s+\frac{3}{2}}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}\quad\text{and}\quad\|\gamma(\varkappa)\|_{X^{s+\frac{3}{2}}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2},

which follow from (
2.9
), (
7.2
), (
3.33
), and (
3.35
).

As
\varkappa
is fixed, we allow implicit constants to depend on this parameter. Throughout the proof, we will take
\kappa\geq 2\varkappa
. When it is convenient to argue by duality, we will write
\phi
for a generic function in
L^{\infty}_{t}H^{4}
of unit norm.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{1}
.
We apply the estimate (
3.24
) to bound

\left\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{1}\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}\lesssim\kappa^{-2}\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}\lesssim\kappa^{-2}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{2}
.
Similarly, using duality and (
3.27
), we may bound

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{2}\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{12}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}\bigl\|\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{-(s+1)}}\lesssim\kappa^{-2(1+s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{3}
.
We estimate

\displaystyle\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{12}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{2}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)][\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}]q\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}.

We will bound both of these terms using duality. Using (
7.3
) and (
7.4
), we get

\displaystyle\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{2}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\phi}\|\phi[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\|_{X^{s+\frac{3}{2}}_{\kappa}}\|q\|_{X^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}_{\kappa}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl[\kappa^{-1}+\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\bigr]\kappa^{-\frac{2}{3}(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{2}{3}(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

Using instead (
3.27
) and (
2.11
), we may bound

\displaystyle\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)][\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}]q\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sup_{\phi}\|\psi_{h}^{6}\phi[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s+1}}\bigl\|[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}]q\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{-(s+1)}}\lesssim\kappa^{-(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{4}
.
Using
L^{1}\subset H^{-4}
and
H^{s+1}\subset L^{\infty}
together with (
3.24
), we get

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{4}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\bigl\|\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\kappa^{-2(1+s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{5}
.
Arguing as for
\mathbf{err}_{4}
, we may bound

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{5}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}\lesssim\kappa^{2}\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\bigl\|\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\bigl\|\tfrac{\partial q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{6}
.
Using that
L^{1}\subset H^{-4}
and Corollary
5.7
we may bound

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{6}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{t,x}^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{2}{3}(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{7}
.
We estimate

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{7}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}g_{12}(\varkappa)\partial^{2}\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa^{2}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}g_{12}(\varkappa)[\psi_{h}^{6},\partial^{2}]\bigl[\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}.

Using that
L^{1}\subset H^{-4}
, we estimate the commutator term by

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}g_{12}(\varkappa)[\psi_{h}^{6},\partial^{2}]\bigl[\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{2}\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\bigl\|\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\bigl\|\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{2}}\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

To estimate the remaining term, we argue by duality. Using (
7.4
), we have

(7.5)

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}g_{12}(\varkappa)\partial^{2}\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{2}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}\bigl\|\sqrt{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigl[\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{-(s+\frac{1}{2})}}

Employing Lemma
2.8
, breaking into Littlewood–Paley pieces, and using Bernstein’s inequality, we deduce that

LHS(
7.5
)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}\!\!\!\sum_{N_{2}\leq N_{1}}\!\!\!\tfrac{\kappa^{2}(\kappa+N_{1}+N_{2})}{(\kappa+N_{1})^{2}(\kappa+N_{2})^{2}}\|(\psi_{h}^{3}q)_{N_{1}}\|_{L_{t,x}^{2}}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}-s}\|(\psi_{h}^{3}q)_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}.

Invoking Corollary
5.7
and evaluating the resulting sum, we ultimately find

\mathbf{err}_{7}\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{2}{3}(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{8}
.
Using
L^{1}\subset H^{-4}
and Corollary
5.7
, we bound

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{8}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{t,x}^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\kappa^{-(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{9}
.
Using (
3.27
), (
3.35
), and (
5.33
), we may bound

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{9}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}\sup_{\phi}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(2\varkappa+\partial)\bigl(\phi[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\bigr)\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{10}
.
Arguing as for
\mathbf{err}_{9}
and using (
5.36
) in place of (
5.33
), we find

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{10}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}\sup_{\phi}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(2\varkappa+\partial)\bigl(\phi[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\bigr)\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{NLS}_{\kappa}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{11}
.
Using (
3.24
) and (
5.35
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{11}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\|g_{12}\|_{L_{t,x}^{\infty}}\bigl\|\gamma(\pm\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}\psi_{h}^{12}\bigr\|_{L_{t,x}^{1}}\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{4}{3}(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{5}.

Collecting all our estimates for the error terms yields (
7.1
).
∎

Proof for (
mKdV-diff
).

It suffices to show the following analogue of (
7.1
):

(7.6)

\lim\limits_{\kappa\rightarrow\infty}\sup\limits_{q\in Q}\sup\limits_{h\in\mathbb{R}}\left\|\frac{d}{dt}\left(\psi_{h}^{12}g_{12}(\varkappa;e^{tJ\nabla(H_{\mathrm{mKdV}}-H_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{\kappa})}q)\right)\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}=0.

Using the identities (
3.12
) for
g_{12}
and (
3.11
) for
\gamma
we may write

\displaystyle-g_{12}(\varkappa)^{\prime\prime\prime}+6qrg_{12}(\varkappa)^{\prime}+6qq^{\prime}g_{21}(\varkappa)+6rq^{\prime}g_{12}(\varkappa)-4\kappa^{2}g_{12}(\varkappa)^{\prime}+\tfrac{8\kappa^{4}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}g_{12}(\varkappa)

\displaystyle=g_{12}(\varkappa)\bigl[\tfrac{8\varkappa^{5}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}+4\varkappa qr+2rq^{\prime}-2qr^{\prime}\bigr]-[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\bigl[q^{\prime\prime}+2\varkappa q^{\prime}+4(\kappa^{2}+\varkappa^{2})q-2q^{2}r\bigr].

As a consequence, we may write (
4.36
) as

\frac{d}{dt}g_{12}(\varkappa)=\sum\limits_{j=1}^{16}\mathbf{err}_{j},

where we define

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{1}=\tfrac{8\varkappa^{5}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}g_{12}(\varkappa),\qquad\mathbf{err}_{2}=\tfrac{8\kappa^{2}\varkappa^{3}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}+4\varkappa^{2}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{3}=[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{3}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}},\qquad\mathbf{err}_{4}=-\tfrac{64\kappa^{4}\varkappa^{3}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}g_{12}(\varkappa)\,\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{5}=\tfrac{32\kappa^{4}\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}g_{12}(\varkappa)\Bigl[\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{\partial r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}-\tfrac{\partial q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{6}=\tfrac{16\kappa^{4}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}g_{12}(\varkappa)\,\tfrac{\partial q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{\partial r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{7}=-16\kappa^{2}\varkappa g_{12}(\varkappa)\Bigl[\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}+\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{\partial^{2}r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{8}=4\varkappa g_{12}(\varkappa)\,\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{\partial^{2}r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{9}=16\kappa^{2}g_{12}(\varkappa)\Bigl[\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{\partial r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}-\tfrac{\partial q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{\partial^{2}r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{10}=-8\kappa^{2}g_{12}(\varkappa)\partial\Bigl[\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}-\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{\partial^{2}r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{11}=2g_{12}(\varkappa)\Bigl[\tfrac{\partial^{3}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{\partial^{2}r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}-\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{\partial^{3}r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{12}=-\tfrac{4\kappa^{5}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\,\Bigl[g_{12}(\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}-g_{12}(-\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}-\tfrac{1}{2\kappa^{3}}q^{2}r\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{13}=-\tfrac{4\kappa^{4}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\,\Bigl[g_{12}(\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}+g_{12}(-\kappa)^{[\geq 3]}\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{14}=\tfrac{4\kappa^{4}\varkappa}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}g_{12}(\varkappa)\Bigl[\gamma(\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}+\gamma(-\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{15}=\tfrac{4\kappa^{5}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}g_{12}(\varkappa)\Bigl[\gamma(\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}-\gamma(-\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{16}=-\tfrac{2\varkappa^{2}}{\kappa^{2}-\varkappa^{2}}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]q^{2}r.

To bound the error terms, we will rely on the a priori estimate (
4.12
) and the local smoothing estimate (
5.22
), which yield

(7.7)

\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}=\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}+\|q\|_{X^{s+1}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

We will also employ the estimates recorded in Corollary
5.9
, as well as the bounds

(7.8)

\displaystyle\|q\|_{X^{-s}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}},

(7.9)

\displaystyle\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{X^{s+2}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}\quad\text{and}\quad\|\gamma(\varkappa)\|_{X^{s+2}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2},

which follow from (
2.9
), (
7.7
), (
3.33
), and (
3.35
).

We will allow implicit constants to depend on
\varkappa
. Throughout the proof, we will take
\kappa\geq 2\varkappa
. As before, when arguing by duality, we write
\phi
for a function in
L^{\infty}_{t}H^{4}
of unit norm.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{1}
.
We apply the estimate (
3.24
) to bound

\left\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{1}\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}\lesssim\kappa^{-2}\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}\lesssim\kappa^{-2}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{2}
.
Similarly, using duality and (
3.27
), we may bound

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{2}\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{12}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}\bigl[\bigl\|\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{-(s+1)}}+\bigl\|\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{-(s+1)}}\bigr]

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{3}
.
We estimate

\displaystyle\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{3}\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{3}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)][\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{3}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}]q\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}.

We will bound both of these terms using duality. Using (
7.8
) and (
7.9
), we get

\displaystyle\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{3}(\psi_{h}^{6}q)}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\phi}\|\phi[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\|_{X^{s+2}_{\kappa}}\|q\|_{X^{-s}_{\kappa}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl[\kappa^{-1}+\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\bigr]\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

To estimate the commutator term, we use (
2.12
) and (
3.27
), as follows:

\displaystyle\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}[1+\gamma(\varkappa)][\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{3}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}]q\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\phi}\|\psi_{h}^{6}\phi[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s+1}}\bigl\|[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\partial^{3}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}]q\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{-(s+1)}}\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

Collecting our estimates we obtain

\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{3}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{4}
.
Using
L^{1}\subset H^{-4}
and
H^{s+1}\subset L^{\infty}
together with (
3.24
), we get

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{4}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\bigl\|\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\kappa^{-2(1+s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{5}
.
Arguing as for
\mathbf{err}_{4}
, we may bound

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{5}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}\lesssim\kappa^{2}\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\bigl\|\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\bigl\|\tfrac{\partial q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{6}
.
Using
L^{1}\subset H^{-4}
and Corollary
5.9
, we may bound

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{6}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}q\bigr\|_{L_{t,x}^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{7}
.
Arguing as for
\mathbf{err}_{6}
, we may bound

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{7}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\bigl\|\tfrac{q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}q\bigr\|_{L_{t,x}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{8}
.
Arguing as for
\mathbf{err}_{6}
again, we bound

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{8}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{t,x}^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\kappa^{-2(1+s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{9}
.
Using (
2.11
) and then (
7.7
) yields

(7.10)

\displaystyle\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-1}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}+\kappa^{-1}\bigl\|\tfrac{\psi^{6}q}{\sqrt{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{s+2}}\lesssim\kappa^{-1}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

Analogously, but also breaking at frequency
N=\sqrt{\kappa}
, we find

\displaystyle\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{-1-s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-2-2s}\|q\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s}}+\kappa^{-2}N^{1-2s}\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}

(7.11)

\displaystyle\quad{}+\kappa^{-1}N^{-(1+2s)}\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)_{>N}\|_{X_{\kappa}^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-1-\frac{1}{2}(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

Combining these bounds, we deduce that

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{9}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\|g_{12}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{s+1}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{-1-s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}{(1+2s)}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{10}
.
Our goal here is to employ (
5.28
). Given
\phi\in L^{\infty}_{t}H^{4}
, we have

\displaystyle[\phi\psi_{h}^{12}g_{12}]^{\prime}\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}=m\big[q,r,\tfrac{(\psi_{h}^{12}\phi)^{\prime}}{\psi_{h}^{12}},\psi_{h}^{12}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)g_{12}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr]+m\big[q,r,g_{12}^{\prime},\psi_{h}^{12}\tfrac{(2\varkappa+\partial)\phi}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr],

where the paraproduct
m
has symbol

m(\xi_{1},\ldots,\xi_{4})=\tfrac{\xi_{1}^{2}}{(4\kappa^{2}+\xi_{1}^{2})(4\kappa^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2})}.

In this way, we see that

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\biggl\|\int\phi\psi_{h}^{12}g_{12}\cdot\partial\bigl[\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\,\tfrac{r}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr]\,dx\biggr\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{4}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}

and thence that

\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{10}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{11}
.
Arguing as for (
7.10
), we first use (
2.11
) and (
7.7
) to see that

\displaystyle\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s+1}}\lesssim\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}+\|q\|_{X^{s+1}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}

and

\displaystyle\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial^{3}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-(s+1)}}\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}+\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q\|_{X^{s+1}_{\kappa}}\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{11}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s+1}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial^{2}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s+1}}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{6}\tfrac{\partial^{3}q}{4\kappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-(s+1)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{12}
.
We first note that (
3.27
) and (
3.35
) imply

\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(2\varkappa+\partial)\bigl(\phi[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\bigr)\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}\lesssim 1+{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}\lesssim 1

for any
\phi\in L^{\infty}_{t}H^{4}
of unit norm. Thus, it follows from (
5.45
) that

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{12}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{13}
.
Arguing as for
\mathbf{err}_{12}
and using (
5.44
) in place of (
5.45
), we get

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{13}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{14}
.
Using
L^{1}\subset H^{-4}
together with (
3.24
) and (
5.46
), we get

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{14}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\|g_{12}\|_{L_{t,x}^{\infty}}\bigl\|\gamma(\pm\kappa)^{[\geq 4]}\psi_{h}^{12}\bigr\|_{L_{t,x}^{1}}\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}-(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{5}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{15}
.
The argument here is essentially a recapitulation of the proof of (
5.47
). For example, from (
5.49
), we have

\displaystyle\kappa^{3}\bigl\|\psi_{h}^{12}g_{12}(\varkappa)\gamma^{[\geq 6]}(\pm\kappa)\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|g_{12}\|_{L_{t,x}^{\infty}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{6}\lesssim\kappa^{-(1+2s)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{7}.

In order to repeat the treatment of the
\gamma^{[4]}
terms given previously, we need one additional piece of information, namely, that
f
defined by

\displaystyle\tfrac{f}{2\kappa-\partial}=\phi\,g_{12}(\varkappa)\tfrac{q}{2\kappa-\partial}

satisfies

\displaystyle\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}\lesssim\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}\quad\text{and}\quad{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|f\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}\lesssim{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}_{\kappa}}

for every
\phi\in L^{\infty}_{t}H^{4}
of unit norm. These assertions follow readily from (
3.24
), (
2.16
), and (
3.33
). Thus we may conclude that

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{15}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-(2s+1)}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{5}.

Estimate for
\mathbf{err}_{16}
.
Breaking at frequency
N=\sqrt{\kappa}
and using (
7.7
), we find

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{6}q\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N\|q\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s}}+\tfrac{\kappa}{N}\|q\|_{X_{\kappa}^{s+1}}\lesssim\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}.

Thus, arguing by duality and using (
3.24
), we estimate

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\mathbf{err}_{16}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{-4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-2}\|q\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s}}\|\psi_{h}^{6}q\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{s+1}}^{2}\sup_{\phi}\|\phi[1+\gamma(\varkappa)]\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-1}\|q(0)\|_{H^{s}}^{3}.

Combining our estimates for all the error terms we deduce (
7.1
), which then completes the proof of the mKdV case of Proposition
7.1
.
∎

8.
Well-posedness

In this section we prove Theorem
1.1
. While we have already established the necessary prerequisites to obtain global well-posedness in
H^{s}
for
-\frac{1}{2}<s<0
, we begin this section with one additional equicontinuity result that will be applied to yield well-posedness at higher regularity.

This equicontinuity relies on a certain macroscopic conservation law, which we introduce through its density

\widetilde{\rho}(\varkappa):=qr-2\varkappa\rho(\varkappa).

That this density satisfies a conservation law follows readily from Corollary
4.14
and the conservation of mass. The associated microscopic conservation law will be essential for proving local smoothing estimates at positive regularity in the next section.

For later reference, we note that

(8.1)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\rho}^{[2]}(\varkappa)=\tfrac{1}{2}\Bigl(q\cdot\tfrac{r^{\prime}}{2\varkappa+\partial}-\tfrac{q^{\prime}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot r\Bigr).

Using
\widetilde{\rho}
, we prove the following analogue of Proposition
4.4
:

Proposition 8.1
.

Let
0\leq\sigma<\frac{1}{2}
. Then there exists
\delta>0
so that for any
q(0)\in\mathscr{S}
satisfying
\|q(0)\|_{L^{2}}\leq\delta
, the solution
q(t)
of (
NLS
) or (
mKdV
) satisfies

(8.2)

\|q(t)^{\prime}\|_{H^{\sigma-1}_{\kappa}}^{2}\lesssim\|q(0)^{\prime}\|_{H^{\sigma-1}_{\kappa}}^{2}+\kappa^{2\sigma-1}\delta^{2}\|q(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

uniformly for
t\in\mathbb{R}
and
\kappa\geq 1
.

Proof.

Using (
3.24
), (
3.25
), and (
3.28
), we get

\displaystyle\|g_{12}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\|q\|_{L^{2}},\quad\|g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-2}\|q\|_{L^{2}}^{3},\quad\|\gamma(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\varkappa^{-1}\|q\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Consequently, using (
3.39
) and (
4.14
) we obtain

(8.3)

\|\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-2}\delta^{2}\|q\|_{L^{2}}\quad\text{and so}\quad\|\widetilde{\rho}^{[\geq 4]}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\delta^{2}\|q\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

whenever
0<\delta\ll 1
is sufficiently small. Employing (
8.1
) and (
8.3
) we get

\pm\operatorname{Re}\int\widetilde{\rho}(x;\varkappa)\,dx=\|q^{\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}+\mathcal{O}\bigl(\varkappa^{-1}\delta^{2}\|q\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr).

If
\sigma=0
, we simply set
\varkappa=\kappa
. If
0<\sigma<\tfrac{1}{2}
, we apply the estimate (
2.7
) to obtain

\int_{\kappa}^{\infty}\varkappa^{2\sigma}\Bigg(\pm\operatorname{Re}\int\widetilde{\rho}(x;\varkappa)\,dx\Bigg)\,\tfrac{d\varkappa}{\varkappa}\approx\|q^{\prime}\|_{H^{\sigma-1}_{\kappa}}^{2}+\mathcal{O}\bigl(\kappa^{2\sigma-1}\delta^{2}\|q\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr).

As the mass and left-hand sides in these estimates are conserved under both (
NLS
) and (
mKdV
), the claim (
8.2
) now follows.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.1
.

In view of the history discussed in the introduction, it suffices to treat regularities
-\frac{1}{2}<s<0
for (
NLS
) and
-\frac{1}{2}<s<\frac{1}{4}
for (
mKdV
). With the tools at our disposal, we are able to give a uniform treatment of both equations over the range
(-\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2})
, so this is what we do. As the arguments for (
NLS
) and (
mKdV
) are identical, we provide details in the case of (
NLS
).

We first consider initial data
q\in H^{s}
, where
-\frac{1}{2}<s<0
. Let
0<\delta\ll 1
be sufficiently small and, rescaling according to (
1.3
), assume that
q\in B_{\delta}
. Let
\{q_{n}\}_{n\geq 1}\subset B_{\delta}\cap\mathscr{S}
so that
q_{n}\rightarrow q
in
H^{s}
as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

In view of Propositions
4.6
and
6.4
, the set

Q:=\left\{e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q_{n}:n\geq 1,\;t\in[-1,1]\right\}

is equicontinuous and tight in
H^{s}
. Further, by Proposition
4.4
we may find some
C=C(s)\geq 1
so that
Q\subset B_{C\delta}\cap\mathscr{S}
.

For fixed
\varkappa\geq 4
let
g_{12}(\cdot)=g_{12}(\varkappa;\cdot)
and
\kappa\geq 2\varkappa
. Let
R\geq 1
,
\phi_{R}
be as in Section
6
, and
\chi_{R}\in\mathscr{S}
be a non-negative function so that
1\leq\phi_{R}^{2}+\chi_{R}^{2}
. We then bound

\displaystyle\|g_{12}(e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q_{n})-g_{12}(e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\|g_{12}(e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{\kappa}}q_{n})-g_{12}(e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{\kappa}}q_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sup\limits_{q\in Q^{*}}\|\chi_{R}g_{12}(e^{tJ\nabla(H_{\mathrm{NLS}}-H_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{\kappa})}q)-\chi_{R}g_{12}(q)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sup\limits_{n\geq 1}\|\phi_{R}g_{12}(e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q_{n})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}},

where the set

Q^{*}:=\left\{e^{J\nabla\left(tH_{\mathrm{NLS}}+sH_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{\kappa}\right)}q_{n}:n\geq 1,\;\kappa\geq 2\varkappa,\;t,s\in[-1,1]\right\}.

By Propositions
4.4
and
4.12
we have
Q^{*}\subset B_{C\delta}\cap\mathscr{S}
, while by Proposition
4.6
,
Q^{*}
is equicontinuous in
H^{s}
.

By Proposition
4.12
and the diffeomorphism property of Proposition
3.2
we have

\lim\limits_{n,m\rightarrow\infty}\|g_{12}(e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{\kappa}}q_{n})-g_{12}(e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{\kappa}}q_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}=0.

Using Proposition
7.1
we obtain

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{\kappa\rightarrow\infty}\sup\limits_{q\in Q^{*}}\|\chi_{R}g_{12}(e^{tJ\nabla(H_{\mathrm{NLS}}-H_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{\kappa})}q)-\chi_{R}g_{12}(q)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim_{R}\lim\limits_{\kappa\rightarrow\infty}\sup\limits_{q\in Q^{*}}\sup\limits_{h\in\mathbb{R}}\|\psi_{h}^{12}g_{12}(e^{tJ\nabla(H_{\mathrm{NLS}}-H_{\mathrm{NLS}}^{\kappa})}q)-\psi_{h}^{12}g_{12}(q)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}=0.

Finally, from the estimate (
6.1
) and the fact that
Q\subset B_{C\delta}\cap\mathscr{S}
is tight we have

\lim\limits_{R\rightarrow\infty}\sup\limits_{n\geq 1}\|\phi_{R}g_{12}(e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q_{n})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+1}}=0.

Thus,
\{g_{12}(e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q_{n})\}
is Cauchy in
\mathcal{C}([-1,1];H^{s+1})
and from the diffeomorphism property we conclude that
\{e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q_{n}\}
is Cauchy in
\mathcal{C}([-1,1];H^{s})
. This yields local well-posedness of (
NLS
) in
H^{s}
on the time interval
[-1,1]
.

From the estimate (
4.12
) with
\kappa=1
we obtain the estimate

\|e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q\|_{H^{s}}\leq C\|q\|_{H^{s}},

uniformly for
t\in\mathbb{R}
and
q\in B_{\delta}\cap\mathscr{S}
. Using this bound we may iterate the local well-posedness argument to complete the proof of global well-posedness in
H^{s}
.

Now consider initial data
q\in H^{\sigma}
, where
0\leq\sigma<\frac{1}{2}
. Let
0<\delta\ll 1
be sufficiently small and
\{q_{n}\}_{n\geq 1}
be a sequence of Schwartz functions so that
q_{n}\to q
in
H^{\sigma}
as
n\to\infty
. After possibly rescaling, assume that
\|q_{n}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\delta
for all
n\geq 1
.

Applying our well-posedness result with
s=-\frac{1}{4}
, the sequence of solutions
\{e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q_{n}\}
is Cauchy in
\mathcal{C}([-1,1];H^{-\frac{1}{4}})
. Applying the estimate (
8.2
), we see that the corresponding set
Q
is equicontinuous in
H^{\sigma}
and hence the sequence
\{e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q_{n}\}
is also Cauchy in
\mathcal{C}([-1,1];H^{\sigma})
. This gives local well-posedness in
H^{\sigma}
.

Employing the estimate (
8.2
) with
\kappa=1
, and the conservation of mass, we obtain the estimate

\|e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q\|_{H^{\sigma}}\lesssim\|q\|_{H^{\sigma}},

uniformly for
t\in\mathbb{R}
and
q\in\mathscr{S}
satisfying
\|q\|_{L^{2}}\leq\delta
. This suffices to complete the proof of global well-posedness in
H^{\sigma}
.
∎

9.
Proof of Theorems
1.2
and
1.3

In this section we prove Theorems
1.2
and
1.3
. We start by considering (
NLS
):

Proof of Theorem
1.2
.

The estimate (
1.7
) follows from (
5.12
) and rescaling. It remains to prove the continuity statement in Theorem
1.2
.

Let
0<\delta\ll 1
be sufficiently small and, by rescaling, assume the initial data
q(0)\in B_{\delta}
. Let
\{q_{n}(0)\}_{n\geq 1}\subseteq B_{\delta}\cap\mathscr{S}
so that
q_{n}(0)\to q(0)
in
H^{s}
as
n\to\infty
and denote the corresponding solutions by
q(t)=e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q(0)
and
q_{n}(t)=e^{tJ\nabla H_{\mathrm{NLS}}}q_{n}(0)
. It suffices to prove that
q_{n}\rightarrow q
in
X^{s+\frac{1}{2}}
as
n\to\infty
.

Decomposing into low and high frequencies, we may bound

\displaystyle\|q_{n}-q_{m}\|_{X^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\|P_{\leq\kappa}(q_{n}-q_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}+2\sup\limits_{n\geq 1}\sup\limits_{h\in\mathbb{R}}\|P_{>\kappa}(\psi_{h}^{6}q_{n})\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}\|q_{n}-q_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s}}+\sup\limits_{n\geq 1}\sup\limits_{h\in\mathbb{R}}\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q_{n})^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s-\frac{1}{2}}_{\kappa}}.

As the set
\{q_{n}(0)\}_{n\geq 1}
is equicontinuous in
H^{s}
, we may apply (
5.13
) from Proposition
5.4
to obtain

\lim\limits_{\kappa\rightarrow\infty}\sup\limits_{n\geq 1}\sup\limits_{h\in\mathbb{R}}\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q_{n})^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s-\frac{1}{2}}_{\kappa}}=0.

Finally, from Theorem
1.1
we have
q_{n}\rightarrow q
in
\mathcal{C}([-1,1];H^{s})
as
n\to\infty
, which completes the proof that
q_{n}\rightarrow q
in
X^{s+\frac{1}{2}}
.
∎

The corresponding result for (
mKdV
), Theorem
1.3
, is proved almost identically: When
-\frac{1}{2}<s<0
, we replace Proposition
5.4
by Proposition
5.5
, whereas at higher regularity we use the following:

Proposition 9.1
.

Let
0\leq\sigma<\frac{1}{2}
. Then there exists
\delta>0
so that for any
q(0)\in\mathscr{S}
satisfying
\|q(0)\|_{L^{2}}\leq\delta
, the solution
q(t)
of (
mKdV
) satisfies the estimate

(9.1)

\|q\|_{X^{\sigma+1}}\lesssim\|q(0)\|_{H^{\sigma}}.

Further, we have the high frequency estimate

(9.2)

\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{\sigma-1}_{\kappa}}^{2}\lesssim\|q(0)^{\prime}\|_{H^{\sigma-1}_{\kappa}}^{2}+\kappa^{2\sigma-1}\|q(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

uniformly for
h\in\mathbb{R}
and
\kappa\geq 1
.

To prove Proposition
9.1
, we use the microscopic conservation law for
\widetilde{\rho}(\varkappa)
,

\partial_{t}\widetilde{\rho}+\partial_{x}\widetilde{j}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}=0,

where the current

\widetilde{j}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}(\varkappa):=(qr)^{\prime\prime}-3(q^{\prime}r^{\prime}+q^{2}r^{2})-2\varkappa j_{\mathrm{mKdV}}(\varkappa).

We will first establish analogues of (
5.4
), (
5.7
), and (
5.9
). We then use these as in the proof of Proposition
5.5
to derive (
9.1
) and (
9.2
).

We start with the analogues of the estimates (
5.4
) and (
5.7
).

Lemma 9.2
.

Let
q\in\mathscr{S}
satisfy
\|q\|_{L^{2}}\leq\delta
and let
\Psi_{h}
be as in (
5.2
). Then

(9.3)

\displaystyle\left|\operatorname{Re}\int\widetilde{\rho}(x;\varkappa)\,\Psi_{h}(x)\,dx\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|q^{\prime}\|_{H_{\varkappa}^{-1}}^{2}+\varkappa^{-1}\|q\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(9.4)

\displaystyle\operatorname{Re}\int\widetilde{j}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{[2]}(x;\varkappa)\,\psi_{h}^{12}(x)\,dx

\displaystyle=\mp 3\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}+\mathcal{O}\Big(\|q^{\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}+\varkappa^{-2}\|q\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{O}\Bigl(\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}\bigl(\|q^{\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}+\varkappa^{-1}\|q\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)\Bigr),

uniformly for
\varkappa\geq 1
and
h\in\mathbb{R}
.

Proof.

Using (
8.1
), we estimate

\displaystyle\left|\operatorname{Re}\int\widetilde{\rho}^{[2]}(x;\varkappa)\,\Psi_{h}(x)\,dx\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\operatorname{Re}\int\tfrac{\xi^{2}}{4\varkappa^{2}+\xi^{2}}\hat{q}(\xi)\overline{\widehat{q\Psi_{h}}}(\xi)\,d\xi\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|q^{\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}\|(\Psi_{h}q)^{\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}\lesssim\|q^{\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}+\varkappa^{-1}\|q^{\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}\|q\|_{L^{2}}.

Combining this with (
8.3
) yields (
9.3
).

We turn now to (
9.4
). The quadratic part of the current satisfies

\widetilde{j}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{[2]}(\varkappa)=\bigl(\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}[q,r]\bigr)^{\prime\prime}-3\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}[q^{\prime},r^{\prime}],

where the paraproduct
\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}[q,r]
has symbol

\widetilde{R}(\xi,\eta):=\tfrac{-i\xi}{2(2\varkappa-i\xi)}+\tfrac{i\eta}{2(2\varkappa+i\eta)}.

Notice also that (
8.1
) shows

\widetilde{\rho}^{[2]}(x;\varkappa)=\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}[q,r](x)=\tfrac{1}{2\pi}\int\widetilde{R}(\xi,\eta)\hat{q}(\xi)\hat{r}(\eta)e^{ix(\xi+\eta)}\,d\xi\,d\eta.

Taking the real part we have

\operatorname{Re}\int\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}[(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime},(\psi_{h}^{6}r)^{\prime}]\,dx=\pm\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2},

and hence we may write

(9.5)

\displaystyle\operatorname{Re}\int\widetilde{j}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{[2]}(\varkappa)\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx

\displaystyle=\mp 3\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}+\operatorname{Re}\int\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}[q,r](\psi^{12}_{h})^{\prime\prime}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-3\operatorname{Re}\int\Bigl(\psi_{h}^{12}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}[q^{\prime},r^{\prime}]-\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}[(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime},(\psi_{h}^{6}r)^{\prime}]\Bigr)\,dx.

Proceeding as in the proof of (
9.3
), we may bound the second term on
\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{smore}
by

\left|\operatorname{Re}\int\widetilde{\rho}^{[2]}(x;\varkappa)\,(\psi^{12}_{h})^{\prime\prime}(x)\,dx\right|\lesssim\|q^{\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}+\varkappa^{-2}\|q\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

The remaining term on
\mathrm{RHS}\eqref{smore}
is given by

\displaystyle-3\operatorname{Re}\int\Bigl(\psi_{h}^{12}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}[q^{\prime},r^{\prime}]-\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}[(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime},(\psi_{h}^{6}r)^{\prime}]\Bigr)\,dx

\displaystyle\quad=3\operatorname{Re}\int\Bigl([\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{3}}{4\varkappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}]q\cdot(\psi_{h}^{6}r)^{\prime}-[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{3}}{4\varkappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}]q\cdot(\psi_{h}^{6})^{\prime}r-\tfrac{(\psi^{6}_{h}q)^{\prime\prime\prime}}{4\varkappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}(\psi_{h}^{6})^{\prime}r\Bigr)\,dx.

Integrating by parts, we may bound

\displaystyle\left|\operatorname{Re}\int\Bigl(\psi_{h}^{12}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}[q^{\prime},r^{\prime}]-\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}[(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime},(\psi_{h}^{6}r)^{\prime}]\Bigr)\,dx\right|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}\bigl(\|q^{\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}+\varkappa^{-1}\|q\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)+\|q^{\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}+\varkappa^{-2}\|q\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

which completes the proof of (
9.4
).
∎

It remains to prove an analogue of the estimate (
5.9
). To this end, we denote

{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2}:=\|q\|_{X^{1}}^{2}+\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}^{2},

which corresponds to the local smoothing norm in the case
s=0
.

Lemma 9.3
.

Let
q\in\mathcal{C}([-1,1];\mathscr{S})
satisfy
\|q(0)\|_{L^{2}}\leq\delta
. We have

(9.6)

\left\|\operatorname{Re}\int\widetilde{j}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{[\geq 4]}(\varkappa)\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2},

uniformly for
\varkappa\geq 1
and
h\in\mathbb{R}
.

Proof.

We first establish several variants of the estimates in Corollary
3.5
, inspired by the decomposition (
9.12
) below. Using that

(9.7)

\displaystyle\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|f^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

we obtain

(9.8)

\|\psi^{3}_{h}q\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim\|(\psi^{3}_{h}q)^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\psi^{3}_{h}q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Thus, using (
3.20
) and (
2.31
), we may bound

\displaystyle\|\psi^{3}_{h}g_{12}^{[1]}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

From (
3.28
) we get

(9.9)

\displaystyle\|\gamma(\varkappa)\|_{L_{t,x}^{\infty}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\delta^{2},

and thence using (
2.31
) again, we find

\displaystyle\|\psi^{3}_{h}g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\|\psi^{3}_{h}q\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\gamma(\varkappa)\|_{L_{t,x}^{\infty}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-2}\delta^{\frac{5}{2}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

From the identity (
3.31
) and the estimate (
9.9
), taking
0<\delta\ll 1
sufficiently small we obtain

\|\psi^{6}_{h}\gamma(\varkappa)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|\psi^{3}_{h}g_{12}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|\psi^{3}_{h}g_{21}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-2}\delta{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}.

Consequently, using (
3.12
) we get

\displaystyle\|\psi^{6}_{h}g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}_{\varkappa}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|\psi^{6}_{h}\gamma(\varkappa)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}}+\bigl\|(\psi_{h}^{6})^{\prime}\tfrac{q\gamma(\varkappa)}{2\varkappa-\partial}\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa^{-2}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}+\varkappa^{-1}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|\gamma(\varkappa)\|_{L_{t,x}^{\infty}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-2}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}.

Recalling the identity (
3.39
) and using (
3.11
) to write
\gamma^{\prime}
in terms of
q,r,g_{12},g_{21}
, we may apply these estimates to obtain

(9.10)

\|\psi^{6}_{h}\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}_{\varkappa}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-2}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}.

Using (
3.12
) and (
2.31
) again, we may bound

\|\psi_{h}^{9}g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{t}L^{\infty}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\|\psi_{h}^{3}q\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|\psi_{h}^{6}\gamma(\varkappa)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-3}\delta^{\frac{3}{2}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{\frac{3}{2}},

Using the identity (
3.32
), we estimate

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}\gamma^{[\geq 4]}\|_{L^{1}_{t}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\psi_{h}^{6}\gamma\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\psi_{h}^{9}g_{12}^{[\geq 3]}\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|\psi_{h}^{3}g_{21}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}+\|\psi_{h}^{3}g_{12}^{[1]}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|\psi_{h}^{9}g_{21}^{[\geq 3]}\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{t}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa^{-4}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2}.

Applying (
3.12
) once again we obtain

\displaystyle\|\psi_{h}^{12}g_{12}^{[\geq 5]}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{1}_{\varkappa}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|\psi_{h}^{12}\gamma^{[\geq 4]}(\varkappa)\|_{L^{1}_{t}L^{\infty}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-4}\delta^{3}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2}.

Finally, we use the identity (
3.40
) with the above estimates, as well as (
3.11
) to replace
\gamma^{\prime}
, to obtain

(9.11)

\|\psi_{h}^{12}\big(\tfrac{g_{12}(\varkappa)}{2+\gamma(\varkappa)}\big)^{[\geq 5]}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{1}_{\varkappa}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-4}\delta^{3}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2}.

We turn now to estimating the current. Using Corollary
4.14
, we have

(9.12)

\displaystyle\widetilde{j}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{[\geq 4]}(\varkappa)

\displaystyle=-2\varkappa q^{\prime\prime}\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}+2\varkappa r^{\prime\prime}\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}

\displaystyle\quad-4\varkappa^{2}(2\varkappa+\partial)q\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}+4\varkappa^{2}(2\varkappa-\partial)r\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}

\displaystyle\quad+4\varkappa q^{2}r\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}-4\varkappa r^{2}q\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}-3q^{2}r^{2}.

For the first two terms we apply the estimate (
9.10
) to bound

\displaystyle\left\|\int 2\varkappa q^{\prime\prime}\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\varkappa\|\psi_{h}^{6}q^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}\|\psi_{h}^{6}\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}_{\varkappa}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}\bigl(\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}+\|q^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}+\varkappa^{-1}\|q\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{2}}\big),

which is acceptable.

We bound the sextic and higher order contributions of the remaining terms using (
9.11
) and (
9.10
), as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|\int 4\varkappa^{2}(2\varkappa+\partial)q\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 5]}\ \psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa^{2}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|\psi_{h}^{12}\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 5]}\|_{L^{1}_{t}H^{1}_{\varkappa}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa^{-2}\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2},

\displaystyle\left\|\int 4\varkappa q^{2}r\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\ \psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|\psi_{h}^{3}q\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\psi_{h}^{6}\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[\geq 3]}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varkappa^{-2}\delta^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2}.

It remains to consider the contributions of

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{1}

\displaystyle:=4\varkappa q^{2}r\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[1]}-4\varkappa r^{2}q\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[1]}-2q^{2}r^{2},

\displaystyle\mathbf{err}_{2}

\displaystyle:=-4\varkappa^{2}(2\varkappa+\partial)q\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{21}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[3]}+4\varkappa^{2}(2\varkappa-\partial)r\cdot\big(\tfrac{g_{12}}{2+\gamma}\big)^{[3]}-q^{2}r^{2}.

For
\mathbf{err}_{1}
we use the identity (
3.38
) to write

\operatorname{Re}\mathbf{err}_{1}=q^{2}r\tfrac{r^{\prime\prime}}{4\varkappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}+qr^{2}\tfrac{q^{\prime\prime}}{4\varkappa^{2}-\partial^{2}},

so we may bound

\displaystyle\left\|\operatorname{Re}\int\mathbf{err}_{1}\ \psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|\psi_{h}^{3}q\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}^{2}\Bigl(\varkappa^{-1}\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}+\bigl\|[\psi_{h}^{6},\tfrac{\partial^{2}}{4\varkappa^{2}-\partial^{2}}]q\bigr\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}\Bigl(\varkappa^{-1}\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}+\varkappa^{-2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}\Bigr),

which is acceptable.

Recalling the identities (
3.38
), (
3.20
), (
3.21
), (
3.22
), we may integrate by parts to obtain

\displaystyle\int\mathbf{err}_{2}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx

\displaystyle=\int 4\varkappa^{2}(2\varkappa+\partial)q\cdot[\psi_{h}^{12},\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa+\partial}]\bigl(r\cdot\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr)\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\int 4\varkappa^{2}(2\varkappa-\partial)r\cdot[\psi_{h}^{12},\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa-\partial}]\bigl(q\cdot\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\cdot\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\bigr)\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\int 6\varkappa^{2}\Bigl(\tfrac{q^{\prime}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot r-\tfrac{r^{\prime}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\cdot q\Bigr)\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\cdot\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\int 2\varkappa qr\Bigl(\tfrac{q^{\prime}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}-\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot\tfrac{r^{\prime}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\Bigr)\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\int 2\varkappa^{2}\Bigl(q^{\prime}\cdot\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}-r^{\prime}\cdot\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\Bigr)\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\cdot\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\int qr\cdot\tfrac{q^{\prime}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot\tfrac{r^{\prime}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx.

We then bound each of these terms by applying (
2.31
) with (
9.8
) as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|\int 4\varkappa^{2}(2\varkappa+\partial)q\cdot[\psi_{h}^{12},\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa+\partial}]\bigl(r\cdot\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr)\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\varkappa^{2}\|(2\varkappa+\partial)q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}\|[\psi_{h}^{12},\tfrac{1}{2\varkappa+\partial}]\bigl(r\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\bigr)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}_{\varkappa}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}^{2}\|\psi_{h}^{3}q\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}^{2}\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2},

\displaystyle\left\|\int 6\varkappa^{2}\tfrac{q^{\prime}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot r\cdot\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}+\left\|\int 2\varkappa qr\tfrac{q^{\prime}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\int 2\varkappa^{2}q^{\prime}\cdot\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\cdot\tfrac{q}{2\varkappa-\partial}\cdot\tfrac{r}{2\varkappa+\partial}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\|\psi^{3}_{h}q\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\psi_{h}^{6}q^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2},

\displaystyle\left\|\int qr\tfrac{q^{\prime}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\tfrac{r^{\prime}}{2\varkappa+\partial}\,\psi_{h}^{12}\,dx\right\|_{L^{1}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\|\psi^{3}q\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\psi^{6}_{h}q^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}\|\tfrac{q^{\prime}}{2\varkappa-\partial}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\lesssim\varkappa^{-1}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2},

with identical estimates for the symmetric terms.

Combining the estimates for
\widetilde{j}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{[\geq 4]}
we obtain the estimate (
9.6
).
∎

Proof of Proposition
9.1
.

We now argue as in the proof of Proposition
5.5
, with
\rho,j_{\mathrm{mKdV}}
replaced by
\widetilde{\rho},\widetilde{j}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}
respectively, and the estimates (
5.7
), (
5.4
), (
5.9
) replaced by the estimates (
9.3
), (
9.4
), (
9.6
) respectively, to obtain

\displaystyle\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}+(1+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon})\big(\|q^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}+\varkappa^{-1}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\varkappa^{-1}\delta^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|q\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\mathrm{mKdV}}^{2},

where the implicit constant is independent of
h,\varkappa,\varepsilon
. Taking
\varepsilon
sufficiently small to defeat the implicit constant above and using (
8.2
) and the conservation of mass, we may bound

\displaystyle\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|q(0)^{\prime}\|_{H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}+\varkappa^{-1}\|q(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta^{2}\varkappa^{-1}\|q\|_{X^{1}}^{2}.

Arguing as in Proposition
5.5
and using the conservation of mass to bound the low frequencies, we obtain the estimates (
9.1
) and (
9.2
) in the case
\sigma=0
.

If
0<\sigma<\frac{1}{2}
we first use (
9.1
) with
\sigma=0
to bound
\|q\|_{X^{1}}
and then integrate using (
2.7
) to obtain

\displaystyle\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{\sigma-1}_{\kappa}}^{2}

\displaystyle\approx\int_{\kappa}^{\infty}\varkappa^{2\sigma}\|(\psi_{h}^{6}q)^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{-1}_{\varkappa}}^{2}\,\tfrac{d\varkappa}{\varkappa}\lesssim\|q(0)^{\prime}\|_{H^{\sigma-1}_{\kappa}}^{2}+\kappa^{2\sigma-1}\|q(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

and the proof of the estimates (
9.1
) and (
9.2
) is completed similarly.
∎

Appendix A
Ill-posedness

The key observation that drives everything in this section is the following:

Lemma A.1
.

If
\psi:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{C}
is a Schwartz function and
\psi_{\lambda}(x):=\lambda\psi(\lambda x)
, then

(A.1)

\displaystyle\int\psi(x)\,dx

\displaystyle=0\quad\text{implies}\quad\|\psi_{\lambda}\|_{H^{\sigma}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}\lesssim_{\psi}\begin{cases}1&:\sigma=-\tfrac{1}{2}\\
\lambda^{-1}+\lambda^{1+2\sigma}&:\sigma<-\frac{1}{2}\end{cases}

whereas

(A.2)

\displaystyle\int\psi(x)\,dx

\displaystyle\neq 0\quad\text{implies}\quad\|\psi_{\lambda}\|_{H^{\sigma}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}\gtrsim_{\psi}\begin{cases}\log\lambda&:\sigma=-\tfrac{1}{2}\\
1&:\sigma<-\frac{1}{2}\end{cases}

uniformly for
\lambda\geq 2
.

This follows from direct computation. Better bounds are possible in the
\sigma<-\frac{1}{2}
case of (
A.1
), but simplicity is preferable.

In order to exploit Lemma
A.1
, we need solutions for our flows that initially have mean zero, but later have non-zero mean. For just (
NLS
) or (
mKdV
), this is trivial. However, we wish to consider all evolutions in the hierarchy simultaneously (excepting translation and phase rotation). For this reason, it is convenient to work with the generating function
A(\kappa)
for the Hamiltonians and then expand in inverse powers of
\kappa
. Under the
A(\kappa)
flow,

(A.3)

\tfrac{d}{dt}\int q\,dx=i\int g_{12}(x;\kappa)\,dx.\quad\text{Moreover,}\quad\int g_{12}^{[1]}(x;\kappa)\,dx=-\tfrac{1}{2\kappa}\int q\,dx.

These assertions follow from (
4.20
) and (
3.20
), respectively. Delving further, shows

(A.4)

\displaystyle\int g_{12}^{[3]}(x;\kappa)\,dx=\tfrac{\pm 1}{4\kappa^{3}\sqrt{2\pi}}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\int\bigl(\tfrac{i}{2\kappa}\bigr)^{\ell}\hat{q}(\eta-\xi)\hat{q}(\xi)\overline{\hat{q}(\eta)}\,\tfrac{\xi^{\ell+1}-\eta^{\ell+1}}{\xi-\eta}\,d\xi\,d\eta.

Proposition A.2
.

Both (
NLS
) and (
mKdV
) exhibit instantaneous inflation of the
H^{\sigma}
norm, in the sense of (
1.4
), for every
\sigma\leq-\frac{1}{2}
. Indeed, this also holds for all higher flows in the hierarchy (focusing or defocusing).

Proof.

We first consider a fixed Schwartz solution
u:\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{C}
of our chosen equation. For even numbered Hamiltonians of the hierarchy, such as (
NLS
), we choose initial data
\widehat{u_{0}}(\xi)=a\xi^{2}e^{-\xi^{2}}
, where
a>0
will be chosen small shortly. For odd numbered Hamiltonians, such as (
mKdV
), we choose
\widehat{u_{0}}(\xi)=a[\xi^{2}+\xi^{3}]e^{-\xi^{2}}
.
The key criterion for selecting these initial data and for choosing
a>0
is that

(A.5)

\int u(0,x)\,dx=0\quad\text{but}\quad\int u(t_{1},x)\,dx\neq 0

for some
t_{1}>0
and any sufficiently small
a>0
. The existence of such a
t_{1}
will follow if we show non-vanishing of the cubic terms in the time derivative of
\int u
at time
t=0
. This is precisely the role of (
A.4
).

For even numbered Hamiltonians (i.e.
\ell
even) the integrand in (
A.4
) is sign definite and so (
A.5
) is clear. For odd numbered Hamiltonians, we first symmetrize under
\eta\leftrightarrow\xi
and then under simultaneous inversion in
\eta
and
\xi
; this then leads to an integrand with a sign-definite imaginary part.

In the case
\sigma=-\tfrac{1}{2}
, we choose
q=au_{\lambda}
using the rescaling of
u
given by (
1.3
): One chooses
a
small to guarantee that the initial data has size
\varepsilon
and then
\lambda
large to guarantee that
\lambda^{-m}t_{1}<\varepsilon
and that the norm exceeds
\varepsilon^{-1}
at this time.

When
\sigma<-\tfrac{1}{2}
, we need an extra idea: Consider the solution
q
with initial data

q(0,x)=\sum_{n=1}^{N}au_{\lambda}(0,x+nL).

Note that
\sum au_{\lambda}(t,x+nL)
is almost a solution and becomes more so as
L\to\infty
. As all equations in the hierarchy are known to admit a perturbation theory in high regularity spaces
[
20
,
34
]
, we know that the approximate solution differs little from
q(t,x)
uniformly for
t\in[0,\lambda^{-m}t_{1}]
provided we take
L
large enough. The ill-posedness result now follows by choosing
N
and
L
large enough to guarantee large norm at time
\lambda^{-m}t_{1}
and ensuring that
\lambda
is large enough to place this time in
[0,\varepsilon]
and to make the norm small at time
t=0
.
∎

Evidently this argument cannot be applied to (
mKdVR
), because
\int q
is conserved. Nevertheless, we are able to show the following form of norm inflation in the focusing case:

Proposition A.3
.

For any sequence of times
t_{n}\to 0
, there is a sequence of (real-valued) Schwartz-class solutions
q_{n}
to focusing (
mKdVR
)
that satisfy

(A.6)

\displaystyle\|q_{n}(0)\|_{\dot{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1\quad\text{and}\quad\|q_{n}(t_{n})\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\to\infty.

Moreover, instantaneous norm inflation in the sense of (
1.4
) holds when
\sigma<-\frac{1}{2}
.

Note that at time zero, these solutions belong to the
homogeneous
Sobolev space; this is a stronger requirement than belonging to
H^{-1/2}(\mathbb{R})
and enforces that
\int q_{n}(0,x)\,dx=0
. (Unlike for (
NLS
) and (
mKdV
), this mean-zero property is preserved by (
mKdVR
).) Nevertheless, the (weaker) inhomogeneous norm diverges.

For
\sigma=-\frac{1}{2}
, we show norm inflation for initial data of size one, rather than for arbitrarily small initial data. It is only in this sense that the result is weaker than (
1.4
).

Proof.

All that is required is a careful inspection of the two-soliton solutions

u(t,x):=6\frac{2\cosh(x-t)-\cosh(2x-8t)}{\,\cosh(3x-9t)+9\cosh(x-7t)-8}\quad\text{and}\quad u_{\lambda}(t,x)=\lambda u(\lambda^{3}t,\lambda x).

Fix
\sigma<-\frac{1}{2}
. As rescalings of a single mean-zero Schwartz function,

\|u_{\lambda}(0)\|_{\dot{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}\approx 1\quad\text{and}\quad\|u_{\lambda}(0)\|_{H^{\sigma}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}\lesssim\lambda^{-1}+\lambda^{1+2\sigma}

uniformly for
\lambda\geq 1
. On the other hand, for
\lambda_{n}^{2}t_{n}\gtrsim 1
, we see that the solution resolves into two sign-definite Schwartz solitons (each of width
\approx\lambda_{n}^{-1}
) separated by a distance
\approx\lambda_{n}^{2}t_{n}
. In this way, one readily shows that

\|u_{\lambda_{n}}(t_{n})\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}\approx\log(\lambda_{n})\quad\text{and}\quad\|u_{\lambda_{n}}(t_{n})\|_{H^{\sigma}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}\gtrsim 1.

The claim (
A.6
) follows at once by choosing
\lambda_{n}
appropriately. Norm inflation in
H^{\sigma}(\mathbb{R})
follows via the same summation device employed in the proof Proposition
A.2
.
∎
